# Changelog

All notable changes to this project are documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [Unreleased]

Timestamp format for new entries in this section: `[YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM UTC±HH:MM]`.
Within each category, add newest entries at the top.
Legacy entries that predate this rule may remain without timestamps.

### Added

- [2026-06-08] **Curator leaderboard snapshot via GitHub Actions.** [`.github/workflows/curator-leaderboard.yml`](.github/workflows/curator-leaderboard.yml) runs every Sunday 23:59 UTC (plus `workflow_dispatch`), generates the Galxe curator rankings snapshot, commits [`apps/web/public/curator-leaderboard.snapshot.json`](apps/web/public/curator-leaderboard.snapshot.json), and pushes to `main` to trigger the existing Vercel deploy. The credential route falls back to `{NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL}/curator-leaderboard.snapshot.json` when `CURATOR_LEADERBOARD_SNAPSHOT_URL` is unset.

- [2026-06-08] **Curators Guild Galxe campaign (soft launch).** [`growth/GALXE_CAMPAIGN_PLAN.md`](growth/GALXE_CAMPAIGN_PLAN.md) documents the Curators-only soft launch: Genesis entry quest, weekly Top 5 NAV-growth payouts ($50/week via Galxe USDC claim), streak badges, and four OATs (Genesis Curator, Weekly Champion, Consistent Curator, Veteran Curator). Other guilds (Educators, Allocators, Engineers) deferred to Season 2. Engineering: Galxe REST credential route at [`apps/web/src/app/api/galxe/credential/route.ts`](apps/web/src/app/api/galxe/credential/route.ts), shared curator logic under [`apps/web/src/lib/galxe/`](apps/web/src/lib/galxe/), and weekly snapshot worker at [`services/leaderboard-worker/`](services/leaderboard-worker/). Operator: deploy the credential route, run the worker on a Sunday cron, and set `CURATOR_LEADERBOARD_SNAPSHOT_URL` on Vercel.

- [2026-05-27 14:20 UTC+05:30] **Web app deploy-update recovery + production loading UX pass (core routes).** The PWA/service-worker path now runs **fresh-first** to prevent stale HTML/chunk mismatch loops after deploys: [`apps/web/public/sw.js`](apps/web/public/sw.js) no longer caches navigations/documents, keeps only safe static-asset stale-while-revalidate behavior, and adds SW message handling for `SKIP_WAITING`; stamp placeholder alignment was fixed by switching cache versioning to `__SW_BUILD_STAMP__` for [`scripts/stamp-sw-version.mjs`](scripts/stamp-sw-version.mjs). [`apps/web/src/components/pwa/pwa-bootstrap.tsx`](apps/web/src/components/pwa/pwa-bootstrap.tsx) now detects waiting workers (`updatefound`/`installed` + existing waiting registration), renders a persistent top update banner, and performs one-click controlled activation+reload. Added app-boot/chunk mismatch recovery surfaces so users never see a blank page: [`apps/web/src/components/pwa/boot-recovery-guard.tsx`](apps/web/src/components/pwa/boot-recovery-guard.tsx) catches recoverable chunk-load failures and shows Retry/Hard refresh; [`apps/web/src/app/global-error.tsx`](apps/web/src/app/global-error.tsx) now renders actionable recovery UI instead of a generic Next error shell. Added global route transition feedback via thin top progress bar [`apps/web/src/components/layout/route-progress.tsx`](apps/web/src/components/layout/route-progress.tsx), wired in root layout [`apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx`](apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx). Added shared structured loading primitives [`apps/web/src/components/ui/page-loading.tsx`](apps/web/src/components/ui/page-loading.tsx) plus route-level `loading.tsx` for core user routes: [`dashboard`](apps/web/src/app/dashboard/loading.tsx), [`baskets`](apps/web/src/app/baskets/loading.tsx), [`baskets/[address]`](apps/web/src/app/baskets/[address]/loading.tsx), [`portfolio`](apps/web/src/app/portfolio/loading.tsx), [`prices`](apps/web/src/app/prices/loading.tsx), [`prices/[assetId]`](apps/web/src/app/prices/[assetId]/loading.tsx), [`settings`](apps/web/src/app/settings/loading.tsx), and [`chains`](apps/web/src/app/chains/loading.tsx). Core loading text gaps were replaced with skeleton states on [`apps/web/src/app/chains/page.tsx`](apps/web/src/app/chains/page.tsx) and [`apps/web/src/app/prices/[assetId]/page.tsx`](apps/web/src/app/prices/[assetId]/page.tsx). Docs + in-app copy sync per Cursor rules: [`docs/PWA_PUSH_NOTIFICATIONS.md`](docs/PWA_PUSH_NOTIFICATIONS.md), [`apps/web/README.md`](apps/web/README.md), [`apps/web/src/lib/wiki.ts`](apps/web/src/lib/wiki.ts), [`apps/web/src/lib/tooltip-copy.ts`](apps/web/src/lib/tooltip-copy.ts).

- [2026-05-27] **LogRocket session replay for the web app (`rvyrh2/indexflow`).** Installs `logrocket` + `logrocket-react@7` in [`apps/web`](apps/web), initializes client-side via [`instrumentation-client.ts`](apps/web/src/instrumentation-client.ts) and [`logrocket-bootstrap.tsx`](apps/web/src/components/analytics/logrocket-bootstrap.tsx) (disabled in `development` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_E2E_TEST_MODE=1`), identifies Privy users in [`logrocket-identify.tsx`](apps/web/src/components/analytics/logrocket-identify.tsx), tracks `LoginCompleted`, `TransactionConfirmed` / `TransactionFailed`, and `BasketCreated`, and mirrors `captureException` in [`global-error.tsx`](apps/web/src/app/global-error.tsx). CSP in [`next.config.ts`](apps/web/next.config.ts) allows LogRocket ingest domains. **Operator:** run `npm install` from the repo root, then redeploy production so sessions record on indexflow.org; review [DOM redaction](https://docs.logrocket.com/reference/dom) before treating recordings as PII-safe.
- [2026-05-27 02:25 UTC+05:30] **CI vault-agent matrix expanded from 2 Sepolia agents to a 7-agent Mantle Sepolia round-robin.** [`.github/workflows/vault-agent.yml`](.github/workflows/vault-agent.yml) `setup-matrix` now picks one trading agent per `:18` tick via `HOUR_UTC % 7` across `mining-manager` (slot 0), `quality-matrix-manager` (1), `rwa-treasurer` (2), `meth-carry-manager` (3), `rwa-yield-router` (4), `funding-rate-harvester` (5), `smart-money-mirror-manager` (6). Each agent runs every 7h (~3.4×/day) — running all seven sequentially under `max-parallel: 1` would routinely exceed the hourly window and queue-block `keeper.yml` / `update-prices.yml` on the shared `keeper-key-serialized` concurrency group. `workflow_dispatch` gained all seven slugs as `agent:` choices plus `mantle-sepolia` as the default `network:`. New env passthrough on the Run Agent step: `MANTLE_SEPOLIA_RPC_URL`, `ENVIO_URL`, `BYBIT_API_KEY`/`BYBIT_API_SECRET`/`BYBIT_TESTNET` (default `1`), `NANSEN_API_KEY`. **Operator:** set the missing secrets (`MANTLE_SEPOLIA_RPC_URL`, `ENVIO_URL`; optional `BYBIT_*` / `NANSEN_API_KEY`) on `reubenr0d/indexflow-prototype` before re-enabling the cron tick; without `MANTLE_SEPOLIA_RPC_URL` every agent in the rotation fails fast on its first `cast call`. The four optional secrets degrade cleanly (each agent's frontmatter declares the fallback mode). [`agents/mining-manager.md`](agents/mining-manager.md) and [`agents/quality-matrix-manager.md`](agents/quality-matrix-manager.md) frontmatter now declare `network: mantle-sepolia` to match the CI value; `quality-matrix-manager.autoAllocateTargetBps` lowered from `5000` → `3000` per the hackathon plan.
- [2026-05-27 02:25 UTC+05:30] **Three new MCP servers + skills land for the Mantle round-robin.** [`apps/mcps/bybit/`](apps/mcps/bybit/) (read-only V5 perp quotes + funding history, testnet by default, no auth required for the V5 market endpoints), [`apps/mcps/nansen/`](apps/mcps/nansen/) (smart-money holdings + token anomaly with an Envio-derived Mantle DEX swap fallback when `NANSEN_API_KEY` is unset — `nansen_mode: "envio_only"` flagged in every degraded response, confidence tier capped at `medium`), and [`apps/mcps/rwa-adapter/`](apps/mcps/rwa-adapter/) (read tools `get_reserve_state` / `get_pending_redemptions` / `get_yield_landscape` / `simulate_*`, write tools `allocate_to_rwa` / `withdraw_from_rwa` / `harvest_rwa_yield` / `set_reserve_token` / `rebalance_reserve` routed through `BasketVault` so the adapter's `onlyVault` modifier holds). All three carry `--smoke` modes and per-package unit tests (`apps/mcps/bybit/symbol-mapping.test.mjs`, `apps/mcps/nansen/confidence-tier.test.mjs`, `apps/mcps/rwa-adapter/simulate.test.mjs` — 16 tests total). Registered in [`agents/mcp-servers.json`](agents/mcp-servers.json) with explicit `envPassthrough` lists; companion skill files at [`agents/skills/rwa-adapter.md`](agents/skills/rwa-adapter.md), [`agents/skills/bybit.md`](agents/skills/bybit.md), [`agents/skills/nansen.md`](agents/skills/nansen.md) document tool ordering, agent-boundary rules (e.g. `rwa-treasurer` may never call `set_reserve_token` — that boundary is `rwa-yield-router`-only), and degraded-mode fallback policy.
- [2026-05-27 02:25 UTC+05:30] **`BasketVault` RWA hooks ship.** New methods: `setRWAAdapter(address)` (rejects adapters whose `vault()` ≠ `address(this)`), `setRWATargetBps(uint256)`, `allocateToRWA(uint256)`, `withdrawFromRWA(uint256)`, `harvestRWAYield()` (permissionless NAV refresh), `rotateReserveToken(uint8)`. The adapter is per-vault (the adapter's `onlyVault` modifier means the keeper cannot bypass the vault wrapper); `_totalVaultValue()` now includes `rwaAdapter.getReserveValueUsdc()` so NAV reflects reserve holdings. A new `pendingRedemptionsUsdc` counter tracks the open redemption queue total (incremented on `RedemptionQueued`, decremented on `RedemptionProcessed`) so `rwa-treasurer` can size the redemption-margin floor without iterating the queue. Events: `RWAAdapterSet`, `RWATargetBpsUpdated`, `AllocatedToRWA(usdcAmount, reserveOut)`, `WithdrawnFromRWA(usdcAmount)`, `ReserveTokenRotated(oldToken, newToken)`, `RWAYieldHarvested(reserveValueUsdc)`. All 32 existing BasketVault tests still pass; [`apps/web/src/abi/`](apps/web/src/abi/) and [`apps/envio/abis/`](apps/envio/abis/) regenerated via `forge build` + `scripts/extract-abis.js`.
- [2026-05-27 02:25 UTC+05:30] **`Deploy.s.sol` learns the Mantle RWA testnet stack.** A new `_deployRWA(deployed, deployer)` step runs only when `CHAIN=mantle-sepolia` and deploys the testnet mocks (`MockUSDY` + `MockUSDYInstantManager`, `MockMUSD` + `MockMUSDWrapper`, `MockMETH` + `MethAdapter`), then `configureAsset`s `USDY-USDC` and `METH-USDC` as `CustomRelayer` feeds on the `OracleAdapter` so the keeper can post real off-chain prices (no hardcoded yields or admin-set price curves anywhere in the stack). The `_buildJson` writer emits a new `rwa: { usdy, usdyManager, musd, musdWrapper, meth, methAdapter }` block plus `rwaAssetIds: { USDY, METH }` (both `null` when not deployed on the target chain). Per-vault `RWAReserveAdapter` deployment is intentionally NOT done in `Deploy.s.sol` because the adapter pins its `vault` address at construction — the per-vault wiring is operator-driven (or a future BasketFactory extension). [`test/DeployScripts.t.sol`](test/DeployScripts.t.sol) covers both the null-RWA shape and the populated shape. **Operator action required (todo 6 of the CI wiring plan):** `CHAIN=mantle-sepolia forge script Deploy.s.sol --rpc-url mantle_sepolia --broadcast` to actually deploy the new RWA primitives; refresh [`apps/web/src/config/mantle-sepolia-deployment.json`](apps/web/src/config/mantle-sepolia-deployment.json) with the resulting addresses; update [`AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md`](AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md) Mantle row with the new addresses + `usdy`/`musd`/`meth`/`methAdapter`; start the keeper's USDY/METH price-relayer so the oracle isn't stale. Until that happens the three RWA agents (`rwa-treasurer`, `meth-carry-manager`, `rwa-yield-router`) will fail fast with `NO_ADAPTER` from `rwa-adapter-mcp.get_reserve_state` — by design, since blind RWA writes against an unwired vault would silently route nowhere.
- [2026-05-26] **Mantle Sepolia hub deployed (hackathon item 1).** Added `mantle-sepolia` to [`config/chains.json`](config/chains.json) (chain `5003`, CCIP selector `8236463271206331221`, `role: hub`), Foundry/web/keeper wiring, [`apps/web/src/config/mantle-sepolia-deployment.json`](apps/web/src/config/mantle-sepolia-deployment.json) at block `39077789` (`BasketFactory` `0xa07a1820…f318C`, full perp stack + `StateRelay` `0xf4aFB676…2AB5`), Envio network `5003` in [`apps/envio/config.yaml`](apps/envio/config.yaml), and default web target `mantle-sepolia`. Test MNT was sourced by `mint(uint256)` on Sepolia MNT `0x65e37B558F64E2Be5768DB46DF22F93d85741A9E` and `depositMNTTo` via Mantle L1 bridge `0x21F308067241B2028503c07bd7cB3751FFab0Fb2` (not the generic OP `0x61525…` bridge).

### Changed

- [2026-06-06 18:30 UTC+05:30] **Basket detail AI decisions are collapsed by default.** The `/baskets/<vault>` AI Operator card now keeps historical run details hidden until the user clicks "Show AI decisions", with README/docs and tooltip copy updated to match.

- [2026-06-05 18:10 UTC+05:30] **Codex repo rules now explicitly include applicable Cursor agent rules.** [`AGENTS.md`](AGENTS.md) tells Codex to check `.cursor/rules/*.mdc` before edits, apply matching Cursor rules alongside repo instructions, and defer to `AGENTS.md` if the two conflict.

- [2026-05-26] **Envio HyperIndex URL rotated to `115a80f` (Mantle Sepolia multichain).** After the Mantle Sepolia hub deploy and `apps/envio/config.yaml` network `5003` push, Envio Cloud redeployed at `https://indexer.dev.hyperindex.xyz/115a80f/v1/graphql`. Updated [`AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md`](AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md), [`COMPANY.md`](COMPANY.md), envio-graphql skill/tests, and [`apps/envio/src/utils/contractCalls.ts`](apps/envio/src/utils/contractCalls.ts) (`MANTLE_SEPOLIA_RPC_URL` / chain `5003`). **Operator:** set Vercel `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENVIO_URL`, `apps/web/.env.local`, GitHub `ENVIO_URL`, and Envio Cloud RPC env vars to match; retire slug `822ce13`.

- [2026-05-26 23:00 UTC+05:30] **Navbar "Mint 10,000 Test USDC" only on protocol app routes.** The testnet faucet in [`apps/web/src/components/layout/header.tsx`](apps/web/src/components/layout/header.tsx) was visible on every page except `/` (`isTestnet && !onHome`), so it appeared on `/blog`, `/docs`, `/ops`, etc. It now uses `isAppRoute()` and only renders under `/baskets`, `/prices`, `/chains`, `/portfolio`, `/settings`, `/admin`, and `/dashboard` (desktop + mobile nav).

- [2026-05-26 22:30 UTC+05:30] **`/ops` Strategic priorities: hide metric line on cards.** Success metrics stay in [`COMPANY.md`](COMPANY.md) for manifest completeness; [`apps/web/src/app/ops/page.tsx`](apps/web/src/app/ops/page.tsx) no longer renders the Gauge row.

- [2026-05-26 22:15 UTC+05:30] **`/ops` Strategic priorities: consistent goal descriptions.** Each goal in [`COMPANY.md`](COMPANY.md) §Strategic priorities now has a `description` (what we are optimizing for) and a normalized `metric` (how we measure it). [`apps/web/src/lib/ops.server.ts`](apps/web/src/lib/ops.server.ts) maps `description` / legacy `rationale` and coerces list metrics to a single string; [`apps/web/src/app/ops/page.tsx`](apps/web/src/app/ops/page.tsx) renders the description under the card title.

- [2026-05-26 21:45 UTC+05:30] **`/ops` Overview: drop the Active partnerships card from Strategic priorities.** The `partnership-pipeline` goal stays in [`COMPANY.md`](COMPANY.md) for manifest completeness, but [`apps/web/src/lib/ops.server.ts`](apps/web/src/lib/ops.server.ts) now filters it out of `strategicPriorities` — partnerships already have a dedicated pipeline strip on [`/ops/growth`](apps/web/src/app/ops/growth/page.tsx).

- [2026-05-26 21:30 UTC+05:30] **`/ops` public surface renamed for clearer positioning.** Eyebrow is now **Live Manifest · v0.3**, H1 **IndexFlow AI DAO**, subtitle echoes manifest/heartbeats in git. Header and footer nav both link to `/ops` as **Agent Ops** (was "Agents" / "Agent Company"). SEO metadata on [`apps/web/src/app/ops/page.tsx`](apps/web/src/app/ops/page.tsx) and site-wide description in [`apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx`](apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx) updated to match. [`ops-hero.tsx`](apps/web/src/components/ops/ops-hero.tsx) synced for consistency (component unused in layout). Internal docs (`COMPANY.md`, blog, agent prompts) still use "Agent Company" as the canonical term.

### Fixed

- [2026-06-05 23:08 UTC+05:30] **Issue/meta agents no longer get forced into vault deployment.** [`scripts/agent-runner.mjs`](scripts/agent-runner.mjs) now scopes vault lifecycle prompts and final-summary `create_vault` enforcement to agents with `vaultName` frontmatter. Non-vault agents such as `issue-implementer` and `self-improver-issues` keep state/run-log/heartbeat memory without receiving `## Your Vault` instructions or publishing vault metadata.

- [2026-06-05 22:30 UTC+05:30] **Trading-agent vault pinning now covers read/planning tools.** [`scripts/agent-runner.mjs`](scripts/agent-runner.mjs) pins the canonical vault address for vault-bound reads and `plan_open_position`, preventing LLM-mashed vault/assetId hex strings from wasting a turn with `INVALID_ARGUMENT` before sizing. Write tools still append the existing audit note when the runner corrects a vault argument.

- [2026-06-05 20:50 UTC+05:30] **Basket PnL/share-price drift after oracle price syncs is fixed.** Envio now maintains an asset→active-vault reverse index from basket asset updates and refreshes only affected baskets when `OracleAdapter.PriceUpdated` lands, so `Basket.sharePrice`, `basketPrice`, and snapshots follow on-chain NAV after price syncs without refreshing every basket. Added EEE.L/GBp regression coverage in the vault-manager seed guard and Yahoo price updater so raw pence quotes such as `49.049` cannot be treated as USD seeds when the live converted price is around `0.46`.

- [2026-06-05] **Fix LogRocket React bootstrap type mismatch in web analytics bootstrap.** [`apps/web/src/components/analytics/logrocket-bootstrap.tsx`](apps/web/src/components/analytics/logrocket-bootstrap.tsx) now calls `setupLogRocketReact()` without passing `LogRocket` directly, matching the updated `logrocket-react@7` type signature and removing a Next.js build break.

- [2026-06-05 20:05 UTC+05:30] **Fix TypeScript error in Yahoo quote search path.** [`apps/web/src/app/api/yahoo-finance/quote/route.ts`](apps/web/src/app/api/yahoo-finance/quote/route.ts) now normalizes `yf.search()` payloads before reading `quotes`, preventing `unknown`-typed `raw` from breaking strict TS checks at build time and preserving existing search fallback behavior.

- [2026-06-05 18:10 UTC+05:30] **Vault-agent final-summary policy no longer burns enforcement turns on empty vaults.** [`scripts/agent-runner.mjs`](scripts/agent-runner.mjs) now derives entry enforceability from the latest `get_perp_capital_snapshot`: if `availableCollateral` is zero and there are no open positions to rotate, `needsEntry` / stale tracked-set `needsRoll` enforcement is skipped so the agent can emit the intended no-op summary. The run diagnostics now expose `entryEnforceable` and `entryBlockedReason`, and [`scripts/agent-runner-policy.test.mjs`](scripts/agent-runner-policy.test.mjs) covers positive collateral, rotatable open-position, empty-vault, and pre-snapshot conservative cases.

- [2026-06-05 17:30 UTC+05:30] **Yahoo Finance search schema drift no longer blocks valid oracle wiring, and LSE pence quotes now convert correctly.** Internal Yahoo search calls in [`apps/mcps/yfinance/index.js`](apps/mcps/yfinance/index.js), [`apps/mcps/vault-manager/index.js`](apps/mcps/vault-manager/index.js), and [`apps/web/src/app/api/yahoo-finance/quote/route.ts`](apps/web/src/app/api/yahoo-finance/quote/route.ts) now pass `validateResult: false`, matching the existing web search route so Yahoo payload casing changes such as `typeDisp: "Equity"` do not collapse valid search rows into `INVALID_SYMBOL_POLICY` for exact suffixed symbols like `FOX.CN`, `MI6.AX`, and `IRON.L`. Live USD conversion for Yahoo `GBp` prices now normalizes through `GBPUSD=X` and divides by 100 in [`apps/shared/yahoo-usd-quote.mjs`](apps/shared/yahoo-usd-quote.mjs), [`scripts/fetch-yf-asset-price.js`](scripts/fetch-yf-asset-price.js), and [`scripts/update-yahoo-finance-prices.js`](scripts/update-yahoo-finance-prices.js), aligning live quote paths with the existing history-route pence handling. Added coverage in [`apps/mcps/vault-manager/wire-asset-validation.test.mjs`](apps/mcps/vault-manager/wire-asset-validation.test.mjs) and [`apps/web/src/app/api/yahoo-finance/quote/route.test.ts`](apps/web/src/app/api/yahoo-finance/quote/route.test.ts); live verification confirmed all three symbols classify as allowed exact suffix matches and `IRON.L` reports approximately `0.0003` USD instead of the previous 100x scale error.

- [2026-05-27 01:25 UTC+05:30] **Envio HyperIndex slug `31d1482` recovered — Sepolia and Mantle Sepolia are processing events again; Vercel `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENVIO_URL` rotated to the same slug.** Verification on [`https://indexer.dev.hyperindex.xyz/31d1482/v1/graphql`](https://indexer.dev.hyperindex.xyz/31d1482/v1/graphql) after the env-var change: chain `11155111` reports `num_events_processed = 2,594`, `latest_processed_block = 10,928,309`, `first_event_block_number = 10,891,792`, and 4 hub baskets are indexed (`Minestarters ML Picks` `0x4dcd…a0ba`, `Minestarters Quality Matrix` `0xbd7e…41f3`, `Multichain Test Basket 18-43` `0x2c4d…2069`, `Minestarters Precious Metals` `0x4946…d0fe`); chain `5003` reports `num_events_processed = 4` at block `39,140,266`; chain `43113` unchanged at 67 events. **Actual root cause (supersedes the 00:15 diagnosis below):** the `envio` runtime falls back to a hardcoded shared HyperSync token (`3dc856dd-…6e07`, baked into `node_modules/envio/src/sources/HyperSyncSource.res.js` at line `Belt_Option.getWithDefault(param.apiToken, "3dc856dd-…6e07")`) when `ENVIO_API_TOKEN` is unset, and the Sepolia HyperSync `/query` endpoint was rate-limiting that shared token with HTTP 429. Combined with the Rust binary's `ENVIO_HYPERSYNC_CLIENT_MAX_RETRIES = 0` default, throttled requests surfaced as silent empty batches and `progressBlock = -1` instead of a visible error — which is exactly why Fuji (different chain HyperSync, lower shared-token contention) processed events fine on the same deployment. Fuji's success was the misleading signal that sent the earlier diagnosis down the "HyperSync data gap" path. **Fix applied:** the user's personal `ENVIO_API_TOKEN` from [`https://envio.dev/app/api-tokens`](https://envio.dev/app/api-tokens) was set on the live cloud indexer via `envio-cloud indexer env set ENVIO_API_TOKEN <token>`; Envio Cloud hot-applied it to the running deployment within minutes — no slug rotation, no code change required, no config push. The working-tree `rpc: for: sync` override on chain `11155111` added in the 00:15 entry below is now unnecessary and should be reverted before the next `main` push so Sepolia stays on the faster HyperSync path. **Vercel rotation (done in this session):** [`apps/web`](apps/web) Vercel project `snx-prototype-web` had `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENVIO_URL` updated on all 3 environments (Production / Preview / Development) to `https://indexer.dev.hyperindex.xyz/31d1482/v1/graphql`; production redeploy of `snx-prototype-nti91nmjl-…` triggered via `vercel redeploy --target production`, new build `snx-prototype-ku95w1v84-…` in flight (required because `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` envs are baked at build time in Next.js). [`apps/web/.env.local`](apps/web/.env.local) was also pointed at `31d1482` for local dev parity. **Still pending operator action:** rotate the GitHub Actions `ENVIO_URL` secret on `reubenr0d/indexflow-prototype` with `gh secret set ENVIO_URL --body "https://indexer.dev.hyperindex.xyz/31d1482/v1/graphql" --repo reubenr0d/indexflow-prototype` (any CI workflow that builds the web app against `ENVIO_URL` will otherwise rebuild with the old `822ce13` slug). **Durability follow-ups** (file as separate work, not blocking the fix): (a) Envio Cloud should not depend on a working-tree fallback token — open an issue with the Envio team to either inject a privileged token by default for paid/whitelisted indexers or to surface HTTP 429 as a visible error instead of an empty batch; (b) consider setting `ENVIO_HYPERSYNC_CLIENT_MAX_RETRIES > 0` on the cloud indexer so transient 429s self-heal; (c) add a CI smoke that queries `_meta` / `chain_metadata` for each indexed chain and fails if any chain shows `latest_processed_block - first_event_block_number > N` while `num_events_processed = 0`, so this exact silent-drop pattern triggers an alert next time.

- [2026-05-27 00:15 UTC+05:30] **Envio HyperIndex slug `31d1482` (commit `a9bfc32`) was silently dropping every Sepolia event — switched chain `11155111` from HyperSync to RPC primary in [`apps/envio/config.yaml`](apps/envio/config.yaml).** **[SUPERSEDED 01:25 UTC+05:30 — the actual root cause was a rate-limited shared HyperSync API token, not a HyperSync data gap; see the entry above. The `rpc: for: sync` config change documented here was never pushed to `main` and should be reverted in the working tree before the next push.]** Symptoms on [`https://indexer.dev.hyperindex.xyz/31d1482/v1/graphql`](https://indexer.dev.hyperindex.xyz/31d1482/v1/graphql): `_meta.progressBlock = -1`, `eventsProcessed = 0`, `firstEventBlock = null` for chain `11155111`, even though `chain_metadata.latest_fetched_block_number == block_height` (fetcher claims caught up) and direct `eth_getLogs` against `https://ethereum-sepolia-rpc.publicnode.com` proves 2,433+ matching logs exist on the indexed addresses (BasketFactory `0xa9a83c9383da6ea831504f79c68e3230b604145e` first `BasketCreated` at block 10,899,494, plus VaultAccounting `0xc4a01a3eada32f8ebe1cc05407cd7ef9c2dc86d8` 564 logs, OracleAdapter `0x7edb8cf201d1bfd9e028c94c955fcc161978e7ea` 1,808 logs, StateRelay `0xa2dcf0b25fce90b5f5d89add2af1ecc101bbcc7d` 61 logs). Fuji `43113` on the **same deployment** processed 66 events fine, ruling out handler / ABI / address bugs. Diagnosis ladder: (1) confirmed via [`https://envio.dev/app/reubenr0d/indexflow-prototype-3/a9bfc32`](https://envio.dev/app/reubenr0d/indexflow-prototype-3/a9bfc32) that the live slug = current `origin/main` HEAD — not a stale-commit deploy; (2) `envio-cloud deployment logs … --build` returned `null` (clean build) and `… --level error/debug` returned `null` (no runtime crash); (3) `envio-cloud indexer env list` returned `null` (no env vars set, but Fuji works without them so this isn't the gating issue); (4) `envio-cloud deployment restart` returned `status: failed` (likely a recent cooldown). Root cause: HyperSync for chain `11155111` silently returned zero events for this address set — a data-side anomaly the indexer cannot recover from on its own. Per Envio's [RPC fallback docs](https://docs.envio.dev/docs/HyperIndex/configuration-file.md), `for: fallback` only activates on primary **errors**, not silent-empty responses, so a fallback entry would not have helped. Fix mirrors the Mantle Sepolia hack at chain `5003`: added an explicit `rpc: for: sync` block on network `11155111` pointing at `https://ethereum-sepolia-rpc.publicnode.com` with `initial_block_interval: 500, interval_ceiling: 2000, query_timeout_millis: 20000` (smaller initial batch since Sepolia has higher event density than Mantle Sepolia). `npx envio codegen` from `apps/envio/` confirms `Types.publicConfig` for chain `$2 = 11155111` now compiles to `syncSource: { TAG: "Rpc", initialBlockInterval: 500, intervalCeiling: 2000 }`. Trade-off: Sepolia historical backfill goes from "few HyperSync calls" to ~72 RPC calls (~3 min), but it actually works. **Operator action required:** push this config update to `main` so Envio Cloud auto-deploys a new slug (current slug `31d1482` will rotate); verify the new endpoint returns `_meta { chainId: 11155111, eventsProcessed > 0, firstEventBlock: 10899494 }` via `{ Basket(where:{chainId:{_eq:11155111}}) { id } }`; rotate Vercel `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENVIO_URL`, `apps/web/.env.local`, GitHub `ENVIO_URL`, and the `Current URL` row in [`AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md`](AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md) to the new slug. Once Sepolia is healthy on RPC for ~24h and the operator wants HyperSync speed back, the durable next step is to re-enable HyperSync as primary and **add the RPC entry back with `for: fallback`** so silent-empty regressions in the future flip to RPC automatically (still won't help for THIS specific failure mode but will help for the more common case of HyperSync 4xx/5xx).

- [2026-05-26 23:30 UTC+05:30] **Envio HyperIndex build at slug `115a80f` was failing closed because Mantle Sepolia (chain `5003`) is not a HyperSync chain.** Verified against `https://chains.hyperquery.xyz/active_chains` — only Mantle mainnet (`5000`) is in the active list; chain `5003` returns no entry, so HyperIndex couldn't acquire a primary data source for the `5003` network block in [`apps/envio/config.yaml`](apps/envio/config.yaml). Symptoms: `https://indexer.dev.hyperindex.xyz/115a80f/v1/graphql` returns HTTP 404, `npx envio-cloud deployment status indexflow-prototype-3 2f077c6 reubenr0d` returns 404, and both `--build` and runtime log queries return "No logs found" (build crashes before any handler boot). Fix: added an explicit `rpc:` block under the `5003` network in [`apps/envio/config.yaml`](apps/envio/config.yaml) pointing at `https://rpc.sepolia.mantle.xyz` with `for: sync` (per Envio's "RPC as Data Source" docs — required for any chain not in HyperSync). The other three networks (Sepolia `11155111`, Fuji `43113`, Mantle Sepolia `5003`) are unaffected and continue to use HyperSync as their primary source. `npx envio codegen` from `apps/envio/` validates the new config locally. **Operator action required:** push the config update to `main` so Envio Cloud auto-deploys a new slug; verify the new `https://indexer.dev.hyperindex.xyz/<slug>/v1/graphql` returns 200 on `{ __typename }`; rotate Vercel `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENVIO_URL`, `apps/web/.env.local`, GitHub `ENVIO_URL`, and the `Current URL` row in [`AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md`](AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md) to the new slug; and ensure `MANTLE_SEPOLIA_RPC_URL` is set in the Envio Cloud env panel (handler-time `eth_call` enrichment uses it). Public Mantle Sepolia RPC (`https://rpc.sepolia.mantle.xyz`) verified live (chainId `0x138b`, head ~39,135,187 at 17:48 UTC); for higher reliability, the operator can swap to Alchemy/QuickNode by setting `MANTLE_SEPOLIA_RPC_URL` plus extending the `rpc:` block with a `for: fallback` entry.

- [2026-05-26 16:30 UTC+05:30] **New [`scripts/paperclip-configure-adapters.mjs`](scripts/paperclip-configure-adapters.mjs) + `npm run paperclip:configure-adapters` — wires the process adapter (`command`/`args`/`cwd`/`env` secret_refs) onto every active runnable employee after import, so the first heartbeat doesn't fail with `"Process adapter missing command"`.** Discovery sequence: the operator manually triggered `self-improver-issues` from the Paperclip UI and the run failed instantly with `status: failed`, `error: "Process adapter missing command"`, and the only stdout was the secondary warning `[paperclip] No project or prior session workspace was available. Using fallback workspace "/Users/reuben/.paperclip/instances/default/workspaces/a0cf688d-…"`. The fallback-workspace message was a red herring — `curl /api/agents/self-improver-issues` confirmed the agent had `adapterType: "process"` but `adapterConfig` containing only `paperclipSkillSync` + 4 instructions-bundle paths, with no `command`/`args`/`cwd`/`env`. Tracing through `@paperclipai/server/dist/services/company-portability.js` line 2199 (`adapterType: asString(extensionAdapter?.type) ?? "process"`) and `dist/adapters/process/execute.js` line 6 (`if (!command) throw new Error("Process adapter missing command")`) revealed the plugin import only seeds the managed-instructions keys — the canonical `employees[].adapter` block in [`COMPANY.md`](COMPANY.md) (which declares `type: shell`, `command: npm`, `args: ["run", "agent:run", "--", <slug>]`, `cwd: ${REPO_ROOT}`, `envPassthrough: [...]`) is descriptive metadata for the operator, not interpreted by the v0.9.1 plugin importer. Phase 4b of the runbook had this written as "after the agents land, configure each one's process-adapter command + env in the Paperclip UI" but the step was easy to skip and the failure mode wasn't documented in Phase 5 troubleshooting. New [`scripts/paperclip-configure-adapters.mjs`](scripts/paperclip-configure-adapters.mjs) (260 LOC, zero deps) drives the fix end-to-end via 4 sequenced HTTP calls: (a) `GET /api/companies` to resolve the IndexFlow target (override with `PAPERCLIP_TARGET_COMPANY_ID`), (b) `GET /api/companies/:id/secrets` to map each envPassthrough name to its `secretId`, (c) `GET /api/companies/:id/agents` + `GET /api/agents/:id` to compare existing adapterConfig against desired, (d) `PATCH /api/agents/:id` with `{ adapterConfig: { command: "npm", args: ["run", "agent:run", "--", <slug>], cwd: <repo root auto-detected from script location>, env: { ...secret_refs in {type:"secret_ref", secretId, version:"latest"} shape } } }`. The PATCH route at `@paperclipai/server/dist/routes/agents.js` line 2070 merges adapter config by default (no `replaceAdapterConfig: true`) so the managed-instructions keys are preserved — verified by re-reading `adapterConfig` after PATCH and confirming both `command` AND `instructionsFilePath` are present. **Idempotent** — re-running after the agents are already configured logs `already configured (no change)` per slug. Missing required secrets are warned but the PATCH still proceeds; missing optional secrets (`LLM_MODEL_SELF_IMPROVER_ISSUES`, `LLM_MODEL_ISSUE_IMPLEMENTER`) are noted as fine and skipped. Wired into [`package.json`](package.json) as `npm run paperclip:configure-adapters` directly beneath `paperclip:import` so the recommended post-import workflow is `npm run paperclip:import && npm run paperclip:configure-adapters`. Verified working live: ran against the operator's Paperclip instance at 16:21 UTC+05:30, both agents PATCHED (7 secret_refs each, instructions-bundle preserved), re-ran to confirm idempotency (`patched: 0, unchanged: 2`), triggered `self-improver-issues` heartbeat — status went from immediate `failed` ("Process adapter missing command") to `queued` → `running`, confirming the adapter is wired and the child process actually spawned. **Three doc updates in lockstep**: (1) [`docs/PAPERCLIP_RUNBOOK.md`](docs/PAPERCLIP_RUNBOOK.md) promoted the adapter-config table out of the Phase 4b notes into a new **required** §Phase 4c with the configure script as the recommended path + the UI table preserved for operators who prefer clicks; brainstorm-promotion subsection renumbered Phase 4c → Phase 4d + brainstorm step 7 updated to use the configure script + Phase 5 troubleshooting gained a new top row for the exact `"Process adapter missing command"` + fallback-workspace symptom pair pointing at the script + the `ENOENT` and `LLM_API_KEY undefined` rows updated to reference the script too. (2) [`scripts/sync-paperclip-mirror.mjs`](scripts/sync-paperclip-mirror.mjs) extended to generate [`paperclip/companies/indexflow/.paperclip.yaml`](paperclip/companies/indexflow/.paperclip.yaml) — the portable Paperclip extension sidecar with `agents[<slug>].adapter.{type, config.{command, args}}` + `agents[<slug>].inputs.env.{<KEY>.{kind, requirement}}` blocks for both active employees; `cwd` is intentionally omitted (machine-specific) and `env` only declares the requirement (the configure script fills the actual `secret_ref` bindings); as of plugin v0.9.1 the `command`/`args` half isn't fully honoured by the importer either, so the script stays required, but a later plugin build wiring the portable adapter.config through means the configure script becomes responsible only for `cwd` + env. (3) [`COMPANY.md`](COMPANY.md) quickstart step 6 rewritten to invoke `npm run paperclip:configure-adapters` (REQUIRED, with the failure-mode explanation inline) and step 8 promotion procedure rewritten to add the new `ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES` entry to the configure script + a final `npm run paperclip:configure-adapters` step. [`paperclip/README.md`](paperclip/README.md) gained two new sections explaining `.paperclip.yaml` (what it carries, what it omits, why the configure script is still required) and updating the promotion-procedure to include both script edits + the final configure-adapters step. Honest accounting of session-long Paperclip integration friction now totals 7 issues found via real install testing: empty-dashboard-after-onboarding (docs gap), `paperclipai`-not-on-PATH (docs gap), plugin-hot-loads-no-restart (docs gap), Settings-page-renamed (docs gap), `file:///`-rejected (plugin gap), structural-mismatch-flat-agents (plugin gap), UI-plugin-contract-bug (plugin gap), and this one (plugin doesn't apply COMPANY.md adapter block — plugin gap, worked around via the configure-adapters script + portable `.paperclip.yaml` sidecar). The configure script is the bridge from "agents materialized in Paperclip's DB" → "agents actually heartbeat against the shell adapter" — same bridge role the direct-import script played for the earlier UI-plugin-contract bug.

Every issue body is self-contained: the spec is lifted **verbatim** from the matching `COMPANY.md` block (e.g. issue #30 quotes the full 80-line `basket-ideator` brainstorm entry including the 6-step handoff diagram and the `mayDeployContracts: false` boundary), the acceptance criteria includes the mirror-add + COMPANY-flip + sync-import checklist so the operator never has to re-derive the lifecycle, and depends-on lines cross-link the blockers (`broadcast-bot` issue depends on envio-graphql-mcp + twitter-mcp + envio-graphql skill + growth-content skill; `basket-ideator` depends on envio-graphql-mcp + vault-themes skill + envio-graphql skill + growth-content skill). **Label discipline**: every issue carries 3 labels — `brainstorm-promotion` (a NEW grouping label created via `gh label create --color 0e8a16` so the operator can list the campaign with `gh issue list --label brainstorm-promotion`), `category:new_mcp_or_skill` (the canonical [`CATEGORY_ENUM`](apps/mcps/repo-editor/issue-manifest.js) value that best fits all 11 issues — fits agents + skills + MCPs since they're all "new agent capability"; - [2026-05-26 15:25 UTC+05:30] **New [`scripts/paperclip-direct-import.mjs`](scripts/paperclip-direct-import.mjs) + `npm run paperclip:import` — workaround for the v0.9.1 Paperclip UI ↔ plugin contract bug that throws `"Company not found."` on every Import click even when the mirror discovers cleanly.** Discovery sequence: with the structural mismatch already fixed by the mirror (14:55 entry), the operator's "View Contents" panel correctly showed both `issue-implementer` and `self-improver-issues`, yet every Import button click in the UI still failed with `"Company not found."`. Live server log inspection (`tail -100 ~/.paperclip/instances/default/logs/server.log`) showed the reqBody on every failed Import: `{"companyId":"c4b79868-...","params":{"companyId":"repo-db767c14:COMPANY.md","selection":{"agents":{"mode":"all"},...}}}` — i.e. the UI wraps the action params inside a `params` envelope with an OUTER `companyId` that is the Paperclip-side target company UUID, and an INNER `params.companyId` that is the plugin-side source companyId. The plugin worker's `catalog.prepare-company-import` action handler (worker.js line 20065–20069) reads `rawParams.companyId` flat — and the Paperclip server's `/api/plugins/<id>/actions/<key>` route forwards the raw body without unwrapping `params`. Net effect: the worker reads the OUTER `companyId` (a Paperclip-UUID it has never seen) instead of the INNER source-id, so `findRepositoryCompany(state, companyId)` at worker.js line 17422 returns null, and the action throws `"Company not found."` before even looking at the selection. Independently verified by two `curl` reproductions: (1) `POST /api/plugins/<id>/actions/catalog.prepare-company-import` with FLAT body `{"companyId":"repo-db767c14:COMPANY.md","selection":{...}}` returns 200 with the full prepared import source (3 files, ~16 KB of inlined COMPANY.md + 2 AGENTS.md), proving the worker logic is correct; (2) the SAME endpoint with the UI's NESTED body returns 502 `"Company not found."`, confirming the server passes the raw body. The new [`scripts/paperclip-direct-import.mjs`](scripts/paperclip-direct-import.mjs) (235 LOC, zero deps) drives the entire import flow end-to-end via 6 sequenced HTTP calls: (a) `GET /api/plugins` to auto-discover the plugin id (no hardcoding), (b) `POST /api/plugins/<id>/data/catalog.read` to find the source company (default: first one — works because we only ever have IndexFlow), (c) `GET /api/companies` to find the Paperclip target company (default: the one named "Indexflow" — case-insensitive; override with `PAPERCLIP_TARGET_COMPANY_ID`), (d) `POST /api/plugins/<id>/actions/catalog.prepare-company-import` with the FLAT body shape that bypasses the bug, (e) `POST /api/companies/import` (Paperclip's native company-portability endpoint, also what the UI calls AFTER prepare) with `target.mode=existing_company`, `collisionStrategy=replace`, `include={company:false,agents:true,projects:false,issues:false,skills:false}` and the prepared source bundle, (f) `POST /api/plugins/<id>/actions/catalog.record-company-import` with a `params`-wrapped body (this handler DOES get unwrapped by the server) to link source ↔ target in plugin state so the hourly `catalog-auto-sync` cron continues to keep the agents in sync after the initial import. Wired into [`package.json`](package.json) as `npm run paperclip:import`. Verified working live: ran against the operator's Paperclip instance at 15:21 UTC+05:30, both agents materialized (`issue-implementer` and `self-improver-issues`, both `role=agent`, `adapter=process`, `status=idle`) alongside the existing onboarding-created CEO agent (`role=ceo`, `adapter=claude_local`), bringing the `Indexflow` company's agent count from 1 → 3. Idempotent: re-running re-creates the agents with the same content (the `replace` collision strategy is what the UI uses by default). The script is the authoritative reproduction of the bug — it documents the exact wrapping contract Paperclip's server uses for plugin actions, which is non-obvious from the README and the plugin source alone. Two doc updates in lockstep: (1) [`docs/PAPERCLIP_RUNBOOK.md`](docs/PAPERCLIP_RUNBOOK.md) Phase 4 troubleshooting table gained a new row mapping the exact "structured contents visible but every Import click fails" symptom to the workaround script, with a full explanation of the wrapping bug and citations to the worker.js line numbers, plus a new `### Phase 4b — alternative: import via the workaround script` subsection containing the full step-by-step the script performs, expected output, idempotency note, and a 2-row table mapping each mirrored employee to its required `process`-adapter `command` + `args` + `cwd` + env-key list for post-import UI configuration (the bridge from "agents land in Paperclip's DB as idle records" → "agents can actually heartbeat against the shell adapter"); (2) [`package.json`](package.json) `scripts` block gained `paperclip:import` directly beneath `sync:paperclip` so the two repo↔Paperclip plumbing scripts sit next to each other. Honest accounting of session-long Paperclip integration friction now totals 6 issues found via real install testing: empty-dashboard-after-onboarding (docs gap), `paperclipai`-not-on-PATH (docs gap), plugin-hot-loads-no-restart (docs gap), Settings-page-renamed (docs gap), `file:///`-rejected (plugin gap, worked around in §Phase 4), structural-mismatch-flat-agents (plugin gap, worked around via the mirror + sync:paperclip), and this one (UI-plugin-contract-bug, worked around via the direct-import script + record-company-import). The mirror unblocks Discovery; the direct-import script unblocks Import. Together they get the operator from "Paperclip installed" → "agents visible in the Companies dashboard, ready to be smoke-tested" without touching the broken UI Import path.

- [2026-05-26 14:55 UTC+05:30] **Repo-side Paperclip mirror at [`paperclip/companies/indexflow/`](paperclip/companies/indexflow/) + new [`scripts/sync-paperclip-mirror.mjs`](scripts/sync-paperclip-mirror.mjs) — unblocks Import after the v0.9.x plugin's `agents/<slug>/AGENTS.md` requirement collided with our flat `agents/<slug>.md` canonical layout.** Discovery sequence: with the runbook's `file:///` and source-root issues already fixed, the operator's Import attempt still failed with `"Company not found."` and the Discover panel showed **"No structured contents detected"** for IndexFlow. Tracing through the installed plugin `dist/worker.js` v0.9.1 revealed the actual root cause at `classifyCompanyContentPath` (line 18076): the plugin's content classifier ONLY recognises content at five exact paths — `agents/<slug>/AGENTS.md` (plural, capital S, inside a per-slug folder), `skills/<slug>/SKILL.md` (at source root, NOT under `agents/`), `projects/<slug>/PROJECT.md`, `tasks/<slug>/TASK.md`, `issues/<slug>/ISSUE.md`. Our canonical layout has flat `agents/<slug>.md` files (read by `scripts/agent-runner.mjs`) and `agents/skills/<slug>.md` (nested under `agents/`), neither of which the classifier matches — `classifyCompanyContentPath` returns `null` for every canonical file, so the Import flow defaults to a `mode: none` selection across all 5 content kinds and the worker's `findRepositoryCompany` throws `"Company not found."` because the empty-content company can't be resolved against the catalog state. The symlink workaround was ruled out by inspecting the plugin's directory walker (worker.js line 18023: `if (entry.isSymbolicLink()) { continue; }` — explicitly skipped). Three architectural options were considered: (A) mirror directory generating per-slug subfolders as COPIES, (B) restructure the canonical repo (`agents/<slug>.md` → `agents/<slug>/AGENTS.md`, `agents/skills/<slug>.md` → `skills/<slug>/SKILL.md`) which would touch `scripts/agent-runner.mjs` + `scripts/build-issue-context.mjs` + `.github/workflows/vault-agent.yml` + `.github/workflows/issue-implementer.yml` + ~15 docs, (C) skip the plugin entirely and configure agents manually in the Paperclip UI. Operator picked (A) — medium effort, zero runtime risk. New [`scripts/sync-paperclip-mirror.mjs`](scripts/sync-paperclip-mirror.mjs) generates the mirror as a clean rebuild every run (no diff-merge edge cases): copies the canonical `COMPANY.md` to `paperclip/companies/indexflow/COMPANY.md` and the 2 active runnable employee prompts (`agents/self-improver-issues.md`, `agents/issue-implementer.md`) to `paperclip/companies/indexflow/agents/<slug>/AGENTS.md`. The 2 `kind: prompt-only` risk officers (`risk-officer-self-improvement`, `risk-officer-self-improvement-issues`) are intentionally NOT mirrored — they have no heartbeat surface (they're invoked inline by `scripts/run-self-improvement-{risk-officer,issue-risk-officer}.mjs` as system prompts for per-batch LLM calls) and would only confuse the Paperclip Employees panel with non-runnable "Run now" buttons; they stay documented in `COMPANY.md` `employees:` for org-chart visibility. The mirror is **git-tracked** (so GitHub-URL sources work — Paperclip's shallow clone reads the tracked mirror without needing the operator to run the script) and the directory carries its own [`paperclip/README.md`](paperclip/README.md) explaining the rationale, the DO-NOT-EDIT rule, the source-add procedure (use the **mirror subdirectory** `/Users/reuben/Desktop/minestarters/code/snx-prototype/paperclip/companies/indexflow`, NOT the repo root which would discover both the canonical and mirror `COMPANY.md` as duplicate companies), and the promotion procedure for moving a brainstorm employee into the mirror (author prompt → add `{ slug, sourcePath }` to `ACTIVE_RUNNABLE_EMPLOYEES` in the sync script → `npm run sync:paperclip` → commit canonical + script + regenerated mirror as one PR). New [`package.json`](package.json) script `sync:paperclip` wires it as `npm run sync:paperclip`. Four documentation updates kept in lockstep with the new mirror: (1) [`docs/PAPERCLIP_RUNBOOK.md`](docs/PAPERCLIP_RUNBOOK.md) §Mental model gains a fourth ASCII layer ("Layer 0: REPO-SIDE MIRROR") explaining the bridge + adds a third "surprise to expect" (pointing the source at the repo root yields "No structured contents detected" and Import fails — always point at the mirror subdir); Phase 4 split into §4a (run `npm run sync:paperclip`) and §4b (add the mirror as source) with the new source path; Phase 4 troubleshooting gains a new top row mapping the exact "No structured contents detected + Company not found." symptom pair to the source-pointing-at-repo-root fix + an "older mirror cached" row pointing at the UI Sync button; the existing `file:///` row preserved as a sibling. (2) [`COMPANY.md`](COMPANY.md) quickstart Bash block grows from 7 steps to 8: new Step 4 (`npm run sync:paperclip`) inserted between plugin install and UI configuration; Step 5's `Add source:` comment updated to the mirror subdir with an inline explanation of why the repo root vs subdir vs `file:///` triad behaves differently; Step 6 active-employee enumeration updated from "4 active employees" to "2 mirrored runnable + 2 prompt-only documented"; Step 8 promotion procedure rewritten to include the new "add to `ACTIVE_RUNNABLE_EMPLOYEES` + re-run sync + commit mirror" sub-steps. (3) [`docs/AGENTS_FRAMEWORK.md`](docs/AGENTS_FRAMEWORK.md) §Architecture diagram redrawn with the mirror layer between Repo (canonical) and Paperclip server (sync flow + Paperclip discovers + heartbeat + commit-results back); §Setup gains a new Step 3 (mirror generation) with the rationale + link to `paperclip/README.md`, renumbers existing Steps 5/6/7 → 6/7/8, and updates Step 5's employee enumeration to clarify the 2-mirrored vs 2-documented-but-not-mirrored split. (4) [`AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md`](AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md) Paperclip row description gains a mirror callout ("source root MUST point at the generated mirror subdirectory… NOT the repo root") with the v0.9.x classifier rationale and a direct link to `paperclip/README.md`. Net result: the structural mismatch the runbook hadn't named is now both fixed (by the mirror) and documented (so the next operator can't accidentally point at the repo root). Honest accounting of session-long Paperclip integration friction now totals 5 issues found via real install testing: empty-dashboard-after-onboarding (docs gap, fixed in §Phase 1), `paperclipai`-not-on-PATH (docs gap, fixed in §Phase 2), plugin hot-loads-no-restart (docs gap, fixed in §Phase 2), Settings-page-renamed-Agent-Companies→Repository-Catalog (docs gap, fixed in §Phase 2), `file:///`-rejected-by-`looksLikeLocalPath` (plugin gap, worked around in §Phase 4), and this one (structural-mismatch, worked around via the mirror).

- [2026-05-26 14:25 UTC+05:30] **Phase 4 of [`docs/PAPERCLIP_RUNBOOK.md`](docs/PAPERCLIP_RUNBOOK.md) rewritten after the live Import attempt failed with `"Company not found."` — root cause was the runbook recommending `file:///…` URLs, which the plugin doesn't recognise as local paths.** Discovery sequence: operator ran the Phase 4 flow exactly as documented (Settings → Repository Catalog → Add source → paste `file:///Users/reuben/Desktop/minestarters/code/snx-prototype` → Discover → Import as new company), and Import threw `"Company not found."`. Tracing through the installed plugin `dist/worker.js` v0.9.1 revealed two bugs the runbook had ratified by recommending the wrong URL form: (1) `looksLikeLocalPath(input)` at worker.js line 17136 only matches `input.startsWith("/") || input.startsWith("./") || input.startsWith("../") || input.startsWith("~/") || /^[A-Za-z]:[\\/]/u.test(input)` — `file://` prefixes are NOT recognised, so the source got routed through `scanRepositoryForAgentCompanies` which does `git clone --depth 1 --quiet --no-tags --single-branch --recurse-submodules=no <url> <tmpdir>` (line 18331 onward); (2) even if the clone succeeded, it only sees the **last committed** `COMPANY.md`, missing every uncommitted session edit (the operator's working tree had a `M COMPANY.md` modification with all the scope / brainstorm / backlog / basket-ideator / Capital Formation additions that lived only in the working tree at that point). The mismatch between the discovered-company state (from the shallow clone) and the in-memory catalog state surfaces only at Import time as `"Company not found."` thrown by `findRepositoryCompany(state, companyId)` at worker.js line 18544. Three structural changes to [`docs/PAPERCLIP_RUNBOOK.md`](docs/PAPERCLIP_RUNBOOK.md) Phase 4: (1) the URL form table's "Local checkout" row updated to recommend a **bare absolute path** (`/Users/reuben/Desktop/minestarters/code/snx-prototype`) with an explicit annotation that this reads the working tree directly (no `git clone`), so uncommitted edits are visible immediately — meaning the iteration loop is "save COMPANY.md → click Sync" with no `git commit` step required; the GitHub URL row updated to call out that it does a shallow clone and therefore only sees pushed commits; (2) a new prominent **"DO NOT use a `file:///…` URL"** callout block placed directly below the URL form table, citing the `looksLikeLocalPath()` line, the silent-clone behaviour, and the misleading `"Company not found."` failure mode; (3) Phase 4 troubleshooting table gained two new rows — `"Company not found." on clicking Import` (fix: switch to bare absolute path, citing the worker.js line number) and `Discovered company is missing recent edits I just made to COMPANY.md` (fix: same, plus an explanation of why GitHub URL has the same staleness issue without the file:// gotcha). Two more files corrected for consistency: [`COMPANY.md`](COMPANY.md) quickstart bash block (the `Add source:` comment updated from `file:///Users/reuben/Desktop/...` to the bare path with a multi-line comment explaining the `looksLikeLocalPath` constraint and the git-clone alternative); [`docs/AGENTS_FRAMEWORK.md`](docs/AGENTS_FRAMEWORK.md) §Paperclip Integration §Setup step 3 (the inline `file:///…` example replaced with the bare path + the `looksLikeLocalPath` explanation + the GitHub URL alternative with the "push before sync" note). All three corrections came from real Import execution against the actually-running Paperclip server v0.9.1 on 2026-05-26 — the failure mode `"Company not found."` thrown at Import time (not Discover time) is non-obvious enough that the next operator following the runbook would have hit the same wall. Net runbook health: from 0 known operator-friction points pre-install testing to 4 patched (web-UI onboarding clarification → empty-dashboard expectations → `paperclipai` PATH gotcha + plugin hot-load + Repository Catalog rename → `file:///` rejection + working-tree-vs-clone distinction). No code changes; the upstream plugin bug (`looksLikeLocalPath` should accept `file://` URLs per the de-facto user expectation) is left as-is and worked around in our docs.

- [2026-05-23 15:45 UTC+05:30] **Stop truncating MCP errors in the run-log, split soft refusals out of the FAILED-count, add two MCP pre-flight guards, and add a recurring-error-code self-improvement signal.** Surfaced after inspecting the last three `memory(agent)` commits (`949ac12`, `3e02010`, `ab42c05`) where (a) the runner sliced every MCP `isError:true` response to 500 chars, chopping CHURN_GUARD_COOLDOWN / INSUFFICIENT_COLLATERAL payloads mid-JSON and hiding the `recovery_hint` / `assetId` / `recordedCloseAt` structured fields the self-improvement detector relies on; (b) the commit body only showed the FIRST error verbatim, so in `ab42c05` (`8 errors`) the `yfinance_news` validation error was the only one visible — completely hiding three on-chain `Vault: _size must be more than _collateral` REQUIRE_REVERTs behind a bare count; (c) the on-chain reverts themselves were caused by two preventable MCP bugs (`allocate_to_perp` called with a slightly-stale snapshot off by cents, and `open_position` called with `size=1e30, collateral=2.5e8` — below-1x leverage in GMX scaling). Five changes, ~120 LOC + ~310 LOC of tests:
  - **Runner: stop truncating MCP errors + classify soft refusals.** New constants in [scripts/agent-runner.mjs](scripts/agent-runner.mjs): `MCP_ERROR_MAX_CHARS` defaults to **8000** (16× the old 500-char cap; covers every observed MCP error payload — REQUIRE_REVERT cast output is the largest at ~2 KB), overridable via `AGENT_RUNNER_MCP_ERROR_MAX_CHARS` env var when a future MCP starts emitting larger payloads; payloads over the cap are elided with a transparent `... [truncated N chars; raise AGENT_RUNNER_MCP_ERROR_MAX_CHARS to see more]` marker so operators can tell when truncation happened. New `SOFT_REFUSAL_ERROR_CODES = { CHURN_GUARD_COOLDOWN, ALREADY_WIRED, INVALID_SYMBOL_POLICY, MAX_POSITIONS_PER_RUN_EXCEEDED, MAX_SHORTS_PER_RUN_EXCEEDED, INSUFFICIENT_COLLATERAL, INSUFFICIENT_RESERVES, LEVERAGE_BELOW_1X }` set + pure `classifyMcpErrorPayload(content)` helper that returns `{code, isSoft}` — soft is true when the payload's `error_code` is in the soft set OR (`success: false` AND `recovery_hint` is non-empty AND no `transactionHash`). `recordMcpErrorIfPresent` now routes soft refusals into a new `runSummary.softFailures[]` bucket (with the `errorCode` field stamped on every entry) while hard failures (reverts, schema errors, RPC failures) still go to `runSummary.errors[]`. Both buckets persist into the run-log JSONL via the existing `appendRunLog` path (both the happy and failure variants). `create_vault` failures still arm `policyRuntime.createVaultFailedThisRun` regardless of soft/hard classification — even a soft "vault name already exists" refusal must suppress the get_all_vaults fallback to prevent cross-agent vault contamination. Exports `classifyMcpErrorPayload`, `SOFT_REFUSAL_ERROR_CODES`, `MCP_ERROR_MAX_CHARS` through `__agentRunnerInternals` for unit tests.
  - **Commit-message builder: count only hard errors, group by error_code, add Soft refusals tally.** [scripts/build-agent-commit-message.mjs](scripts/build-agent-commit-message.mjs) now reads both `errors[]` and the new `softFailures[]` array from the run-log tail. `parseRunLogTail` exposes `errorCount` (hard only — drives `FAILED (N errors)` in the subject), `errors`, `softFailureCount`, `softFailures`. New `extractErrorCodeFromEntry(entry)` prefers the explicit `entry.errorCode` field (preferred path for the modern runner) and falls back to regex-parsing the JSON error string for older entries. New `groupErrorsByCode(entries)` returns `[{code, count, firstTool, firstError, firstMessage}]` sorted by count desc then code asc for deterministic output. New `summarizeErrorPayload(rawText)` JSON-parses payloads and surfaces the `.message` field so the truncated sample reads "Vault: _size must be more than _collateral" rather than 30 chars of JSON wrapper before the cutoff. New `ERROR_SAMPLE_MAX = 200` chars per error line (wider than the 110-char `JUSTIFICATION_MAX` because errors are the most actionable item in the body). Body now emits `  Errors: 4\n    - REQUIRE_REVERT × 3 — open_position: Vault: _size must be more than _collateral\n    - (no error_code) × 1 — yfinance_news: MCP error -32602: ...` per distinct code, and a separate `  Soft refusals: 3 (CHURN_GUARD_COOLDOWN:3)` tally line when present. Soft-only runs are NOT marked `FAILED` in the subject because no agent action was actually broken. Exports `groupErrorsByCode`, `extractErrorCodeFromEntry` through `__internals` for tests.
  - **MCP fix A — `allocate_to_perp` INSUFFICIENT_RESERVES pre-flight.** [apps/mcps/vault-manager/index.js](apps/mcps/vault-manager/index.js) now (i) validates `amount` is a positive integer string via `BigInt(String(amount))` wrapped in try/catch (matches the `open_position` arg validation pattern, returns the shared `toolError("INVALID_ARGUMENT", ...)`); (ii) calls `castCall(vault, "getAvailableForPerpUsdc()(uint256)")` — the exact view the on-chain `require(amount <= getAvailableForPerpUsdc())` ultimately gates against — and short-circuits with a structured `INSUFFICIENT_RESERVES` payload (no tx, no gas) including `requestedAmount`, `availableAmount`, `shortfall`, and a `recovery_hint` telling the agent to re-read `get_vault_state` rather than rely on a snapshot from earlier in the run. Best-effort: an RPC blip on the pre-flight read falls through to the live `cast send` path so a transient network issue doesn't block legitimate allocations. Catches the exact cents-level rounding bug from `ab42c05` where the agent allocated `2_762_330` raw USDC against `2_762_329.58` available (off by 42 cents from fee accrual since the last `get_vault_state` read). The tool description now advertises `INSUFFICIENT_RESERVES` so the LLM understands the new failure mode without re-reading the agent prompt.
  - **MCP fix B — `open_position` LEVERAGE_BELOW_1X pre-flight.** Same file, fires BEFORE the existing `INSUFFICIENT_COLLATERAL` guard so the agent gets the most actionable error first (sizing bug, not capital bug). New constant `COLLATERAL_TO_SIZE_SCALE = 10n ** 24n` (BigInt for exact arithmetic at 1e24 — `Number` would lose precision); when `sizeBn <= collateralBn * COLLATERAL_TO_SIZE_SCALE` the MCP returns `toolError("LEVERAGE_BELOW_1X", ...)` with the requested vs computed-min figures (`size=... GMX-USD (~$0.0001)` vs `collateral=... raw USDC (~$250)`) and a recovery hint pointing at `plan_open_position` (or the manual formulas `size = collateral * 1e24 + 1` for 1x, `size = collateral * 2e24` for 2x). Cites the concrete contract revert string `"Vault: _size must be more than _collateral"` in the message so the new `recurring_error_code` detector can tie pre-flight refusals to historical reverts of the same logical bug. Reproduces and refuses the exact `ab42c05` reverting pattern (`size=1e30, collateral=2.5e8` — scales to `1e30 vs 2.5e32`, well below 1x). The tool description advertises `LEVERAGE_BELOW_1X` alongside the existing `INSUFFICIENT_COLLATERAL` note.
  - **Self-improvement signal: `recurring_error_code`.** New detector in [scripts/detect-self-improvement-signal.mjs](scripts/detect-self-improvement-signal.mjs) complements the existing `new_error_code` trigger (which only fires the FIRST time an MCP code shows up — a REQUIRE_REVERT recurring 5x across 10 runs was previously invisible after the first). `detectRecurringErrorCodes({runs, agent, network})` scans the last `RECURRING_ERROR_CODE_WINDOW = 10` runs and fires when the same `error_code` appears in `RECURRING_ERROR_CODE_MIN_RUNS = 3` distinct runs (counting runs, not raw occurrences — a single run that hit the same code 5x is one bug instance, not five). Severity tiers via new exported `classifyErrorCodeSeverity(code)` helper + `HIGH_SEVERITY_ERROR_CODES = { REQUIRE_REVERT, TX_REVERTED, INSUFFICIENT_RESERVES, LEVERAGE_BELOW_1X, INSUFFICIENT_COLLATERAL, VAULT_IDENTITY_MISMATCH }` (gas-burnt or vault-safety codes; signal `summary` flags as `HIGH-SEVERITY revert pattern` so `agents/self-improver.md` routes them to the PR channel with `convictionWeight ≥ 0.7`) vs `LOW_SEVERITY_ERROR_CODES = { INVALID_ARGUMENT, INVALID_ASSET_ID }` (schema/validation — `agents/self-improver-issues.md` channel). Deliberately scans `run.errors[]` ONLY (not `run.softFailures[]`) so refused-but-recoverable actions like CHURN_GUARD_COOLDOWN don't inflate the signal — the new agent-runner classifier routes soft codes out of `errors[]` so they're invisible to this detector by construction. Registered in `detectSignalsForAgent` alongside the five existing triggers.
  - **Tests:** 11 new cases in [scripts/agent-runner-create-vault.test.mjs](scripts/agent-runner-create-vault.test.mjs) (full-payload preservation under the new cap, transparent elision marker for oversized payloads, soft-vs-hard routing for CHURN_GUARD_COOLDOWN / REQUIRE_REVERT / generic-recovery-hint / non-JSON cases, create_vault soft refusal still arms the suppression flag, `classifyMcpErrorPayload` pure tests for known codes + transactionHash override + non-JSON / empty input). 5 new cases in [scripts/build-agent-commit-message.test.mjs](scripts/build-agent-commit-message.test.mjs) (free-text errors still surface as `(no error_code)`, groups REVERTs by code with the concrete revert message visible, soft-only runs do NOT mark FAILED, soft tally breaks down by code, `extractErrorCodeFromEntry` prefers explicit field + regex fallback, `groupErrorsByCode` sort determinism). 12 new cases in new [apps/mcps/vault-manager/preflight-guards.test.mjs](apps/mcps/vault-manager/preflight-guards.test.mjs) (allocate_to_perp reads the view, emits INSUFFICIENT_RESERVES with requested/available/shortfall, validates positive integer amount, tolerates RPC failures by falling through, advertises in description; open_position LEVERAGE_BELOW_1X fires before INSUFFICIENT_COLLATERAL, cites the contract revert string, uses BigInt scaling for exactness; pure leverage-math regressions for the ab42c05 reproduction + a valid 2x + the strict-> boundary case). 7 new cases in [scripts/detect-self-improvement-signal.test.mjs](scripts/detect-self-improvement-signal.test.mjs) (recurring fires at 3-of-5 runs with high severity, doesn't fire below threshold, counts distinct runs not raw occurrences, ignores soft-classified codes by virtue of the integration boundary, scans only the last 10 runs, medium severity gets the softer summary, severity classifier known tiers). **All 409 tests across `scripts/` + `apps/mcps/` pass.** No linter errors on any edited file.

- [2026-05-23 15:10 UTC+05:30] **`scripts/agent-runner.mjs` now transparently dispatches codex-class models to OpenAI's `/v1/responses` endpoint instead of `/v1/chat/completions`.** Fixes the CI failure `LLM API 404: This model is only supported in v1/responses and not in v1/chat/completions` that aborted both `node scripts/agent-runner.mjs self-improver` and `node scripts/agent-runner.mjs self-improver-issues` on Turn 1/1 after the 2026-05-23 08:50 commit pinned both meta-agents to `model: gpt-5-codex` — `gpt-5-codex` is Responses-API-only, but the runner only knew how to call chat-completions. Three new pure helpers in [scripts/agent-runner.mjs](scripts/agent-runner.mjs), all exported through `__agentRunnerInternals` and as top-level named exports for unit tests: (a) `modelRequiresResponsesApi(model)` matches the codex family — exact `gpt-5-codex` / `gpt-5.1-codex` plus the dated-snapshot prefixes `gpt-5-codex-` / `gpt-5.1-codex-` (case-insensitive, whitespace-tolerant); trading models (`gpt-4o`, `gpt-4o-mini`, `gpt-4.1`, `gpt-5`, `gpt-5-mini`) deliberately stay on chat-completions so the existing trading-vault path is byte-for-byte unchanged. (b) `translateToResponsesRequest({messages, tools, temperature, model})` lifts the first `role: "system"` message into the top-level `instructions` field, converts user/assistant/tool messages into `input` array items (`{type: "message", role, content: [{type: "input_text"|"output_text", text}]}` for text, `{type: "function_call", call_id, name, arguments}` for assistant `tool_calls`, `{type: "function_call_output", call_id, output}` for `role: "tool"` results), flattens the chat-completions wrapped tools array `[{type: "function", function: {name, description, parameters}}]` into the Responses flat shape `[{type: "function", name, description, parameters, strict: false}]` (the explicit `strict: false` is load-bearing — without it Responses normalises every schema to all-required + `additionalProperties: false`, which would break every MCP tool with optional fields like `wire_asset.seedPriceUsd` or `propose_file_edit.replacements`), passes `temperature` through when numeric, and pins `store: false` so the runner stays the source of truth for conversation history. Subsequent system messages (none today, defensive for future callers) fall through as `role: "developer"` input items. (c) `translateFromResponsesResponse(json)` synthesises a chat-completions-shape `{choices: [{message: {role, content, tool_calls?}, finish_reason}]}` object from the Responses `output[]` array: concatenates every `output_text` block from `type: "message"` items into `content`, maps every `type: "function_call"` item to a tool call with `id := call_id` (preserves the round-trip the runner's existing `pushRejectedToolResponses` / `role: "tool"` follow-up logic depends on), omits `tool_calls` entirely when none are present (matching chat-completions semantics), and sets `finish_reason` to `"tool_calls"` if any function calls fired else `"stop"`. Reasoning items are silently dropped so chain-of-thought never reaches MCP or memory. Dispatch happens inside `chatCompletion` — every existing call site (turn loop, refinement loop, risk-officer, policy enforcement) is unchanged. New operator escape hatch `LLM_USE_RESPONSES_API=1` forces the responses path regardless of model name (useful for smoke-testing the codec against `gpt-5` before pinning a new agent to a Responses-only model); off by default. Existing 429/5xx retry, backoff, and stats accounting in `chatCompletion` are untouched (the 404 from the chat-completions misroute was correctly non-retryable). New diagnostic line in the runner's startup banner — `OpenAI endpoint: responses (source: model|LLM_USE_RESPONSES_API|default)` — makes the chosen path obvious in CI logs without changing the per-agent or global `LLM_MODEL` resolution lines. Tests: 21 new cases in [scripts/agent-runner-responses-api.test.mjs](scripts/agent-runner-responses-api.test.mjs) pin the predicate (positive/negative/whitespace/case/empty cases), the request translator (system → instructions, subsequent system → developer items, assistant text + `tool_calls` → `output_text` message + `function_call` items, empty assistant content with only `tool_calls`, tools flattening with `strict: false`, tools omitted entirely for the risk-officer no-tools path, temperature pass-through, object-shaped arguments stringified), the response translator (text-only → `stop` + no `tool_calls`, function-call-only → `tool_calls`, mixed text + function-call, reasoning items dropped, object arguments stringified, empty/missing output), and an end-to-end round-trip case that asserts the tool-call id flows `assistant.tool_calls.id ↔ function_call.call_id ↔ choice.message.tool_calls[0].id ↔ tool.tool_call_id ↔ function_call_output.call_id` so the existing rejected-tool-response and tool-follow-up machinery keeps working unchanged. All 32 tests across `agent-runner-responses-api.test.mjs` + `agent-runner-model-resolution.test.mjs` (and the wider 290-test scripts suite) pass.

Each draft includes a metadata block (topic, pillar P1–P6, calendar week, source doc path, hook type), the full thread or standalone body, 3–5 standalone extracts (threads only), and a Notes section with image placeholders + brand-voice reminders + partner-coordination instructions where relevant. **Three new services queued in [AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md](AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md) as planned + `owner: user`**: the leaderboard worker (GCP Cloud Run, fuses Galxe loyalty points + Boost.xyz claim state + Envio quality multipliers into a static JSON snapshot the `/operators` Hall of Fame page consumes), the Galxe dynamic-credential REST endpoint (lives inside the existing `apps/web` Vercel deployment at `apps/web/src/app/api/galxe/credential/route.ts` so no new project is needed), and the auto-broadcast bot (X account watching `BasketCreated` events via Envio subscription, brand-sensitive credentials held by the user). All three are `owner: user` per the user's explicit choice that public-facing brand surfaces should not be agent-owned. **README.md `## Growth` checklist** gains the new Content Infrastructure rows for the X plan + Galxe plan + X calendar + partnerships tracker + seeded drafts, a new "Season 1 Operator Trials (Activation Campaign)" subsection enumerating the 12 launch tasks (Galxe space + OATs + REST endpoint + Boost Actions + TBIs + Boost hub-chain support check + `/operators` page + leaderboard worker + auto-broadcast bot + AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY entries + launch thread + season-close Spaces + recap blog), and a new "Partnerships" subsection enumerating the four active partners with status. Backed by the full plan at [.cursor/plans/x_testnet_basket_activation_d9db6842.plan.md](.cursor/plans/x_testnet_basket_activation_d9db6842.plan.md) (the canonical design doc; this CHANGELOG entry is the executive summary). - [2026-05-23 08:50 UTC+05:30] **Per-agent LLM model override (`model:` frontmatter key + `LLM_MODEL_<AGENT>` env var), with the `self-improver` / `self-improver-issues` meta-agents now pinned to `gpt-5-codex`.** Background: the runner previously hard-coded one global `LLM_MODEL` for every agent, which meant the trading agents and the meta-agents (whose entire job is exact-substring `propose_file_edit` search/replace edits over agent prompts and runner JS) shared the same model — and the trading-agent-tuned default (`gpt-4o`) is materially worse at preserving exact whitespace than the code-tuned `gpt-5-codex`. That mismatch was the upstream cause of the `SEARCH_NOT_FOUND` failure in [scripts/apply-self-improvement-proposals.mjs](scripts/apply-self-improvement-proposals.mjs) on 2026-05-23 (the meta-agent authored a paraphrased snippet that passed propose-time validation but no longer matched at apply-time). New pure resolver `resolveAgentModel({agentName, frontmatter, env})` in [scripts/agent-runner.mjs](scripts/agent-runner.mjs) returns `{model, source, envKey?}` with explicit precedence: (1) `frontmatter.model` (per-agent, version-controlled), (2) `LLM_MODEL_<UPPER_SNAKE_AGENT>` env var (hyphens become underscores), (3) `LLM_MODEL` global, (4) hard-coded `gpt-4o` default. Empty / whitespace-only values at any tier fall through to the next. Plumbed through a module-scoped `activeModel` slot that the existing `chatCompletion` body builder reads; `runAgent` calls `setActiveModel(resolveAgentModel(...))` once per invocation before any LLM call, and the startup banner now logs `Model: <model> (source: frontmatter|env-per-agent|env-global|default)` so operators can confirm which tier won. `runSummary.model` + new `runSummary.modelSource` carry the resolution into the run log. Frontmatter pins: [agents/self-improver.md](agents/self-improver.md) and [agents/self-improver-issues.md](agents/self-improver-issues.md) both ship `model: gpt-5-codex` with an inline comment documenting the precedence and the `LLM_MODEL_SELF_IMPROVER` / `LLM_MODEL_SELF_IMPROVER_ISSUES` env overrides; trading agents leave the field unset and continue to follow `LLM_MODEL`. Docs updated: [docs/AGENTS_FRAMEWORK.md](docs/AGENTS_FRAMEWORK.md) frontmatter table gains a `model` row; env-vars table expands the `LLM_MODEL` entry with precedence rules and adds a new `LLM_MODEL_<AGENT>` row; the GitHub Actions Secrets list calls out the per-agent override as optional. Tests: 11 new cases in [scripts/agent-runner-model-resolution.test.mjs](scripts/agent-runner-model-resolution.test.mjs) pin every precedence rule (frontmatter wins; per-agent env wins over global; agent-name hyphens become underscores in the env key; fall-through to global; fall-through to default; whitespace handling) plus two drift guards that assert both meta-agent frontmatters still pin `gpt-5-codex`.
