  - **Idempotent `wire_asset`** in [apps/mcps/vault-manager/index.js](apps/mcps/vault-manager/index.js) pre-checks `OracleAdapter.getAssetConfig(keccak256(symbol))` via a new `lookupOracleAssetBySymbol` helper that uses `runCast(["keccak", symbol])` (no hashing dep) and parses the trailing `(true|false)` of the cast tuple to detect `active`. When the symbol is already active it returns a structured `{success:false, error_code:"ALREADY_WIRED", symbol, assetId, recovery_hint}` short-circuit before the cast send fires. Smoke-verified end-to-end against Sepolia: `wire_asset({symbol:"CRML", seedPriceUsd:0.045})` now returns `error_code: ALREADY_WIRED` with `assetId: 0x1a3a155cd907f30cc1c6c5c4905e1cb13e9dee01b8f7d9ca3e5e6401cd71775f`.
  - **Custom-error revert decoder** in new [apps/mcps/vault-manager/revert-decoder.mjs](apps/mcps/vault-manager/revert-decoder.mjs) maps 14 selectors (`MappingAlreadyExists`/`MappingNotFound`/`ZeroAddress` from PriceSync; `VaultNotRegistered`/`VaultAlreadyRegistered`/`InsufficientCapital`/`AssetTokenNotMapped`/`PositionNotFound` from VaultAccounting; `AssetNotFound`/`AssetNotActive`/`InvalidPrice`/`Unauthorized`/`StalePrice`/`DeviationTooLarge` from OracleAdapter) plus `Error(string)` + `Panic(uint256)` to structured `error_code` payloads with decoded args (e.g. `INSUFFICIENT_CAPITAL { vault, requested, available }`). Selector list is hand-curated and pinned by a `cast sig` parity test so it can't drift. `writeError()` now invokes `decodeCastRevert(stderr)` and surfaces the decoded payload before falling back to the legacy `TX_REVERTED`/`TX_FAILED` branches.
  - **Address / bytes32 input validation** via new [apps/mcps/vault-manager/address-validation.mjs](apps/mcps/vault-manager/address-validation.mjs) (`ADDRESS_RE = /^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$/`, `BYTES32_RE = /^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$/`, `validateAddress`/`validateBytes32`/`validateArgs`). Every read+write tool that takes a `vault` or `assetId` (`get_vault_state`, `get_vault_pnl`, `list_open_positions`, `get_position_tracking`, `set_vault_assets`, `allocate_to_perp`, `withdraw_from_perp`, `open_position`, `close_position`) now runs `checkArgs([...])` at the handler entry and returns `{success:false, error_code:"INVALID_ARGUMENT", argName, argKind, argValue, recovery_hint}` instead of letting `cast` choke on a malformed hex string. Specifically rejects the exact 28-byte mash from the 2026-05-22 quality-matrix-manager incident.
  - **Vault-arg pinning at the runner** in [scripts/agent-runner.mjs](scripts/agent-runner.mjs) via a new pure helper `applyVaultArgPin({toolName, args, canonicalVault})` exported through `__agentRunnerInternals` alongside `VAULT_ARG_WRITE_TOOLS = {set_vault_assets, allocate_to_perp, withdraw_from_perp, open_position, close_position}`. `executeToolCall` invokes the helper before calling the MCP; when the LLM-supplied `args.vault` differs (case-insensitive) from `capturedVaultAddress`, the runner overrides it with the canonical vault, appends a `[runner: pinned vault to canonical …]` note to `args.justification`, logs `[POLICY] Overrode hallucinated vault arg <bad> -> <canonical>`, and records the override under `runSummary.policyDiagnostics.vaultPinOverrides[]` so it surfaces in run summaries. Read-tool calls (`get_vault_state` etc.) deliberately skip this so a future "poll a sibling vault" gesture isn't blocked.
  - **`open_position` / `close_position` pre-flight** in `apps/mcps/vault-manager/index.js`. New `readVaultAccountingState(d, vault)` mirrors the on-chain `_availableCapital = max(0, depositedCapital + realisedPnL - collateralLocked)` calc; `open_position` now short-circuits with `{success:false, error_code:"INSUFFICIENT_COLLATERAL", requestedCollateral, availableCollateral, depositedCapital, collateralLocked, realisedPnL, positionCount, recovery_hint}` when `BigInt(collateral) > available`. `close_position` does the analogous pre-flight against `getPositionTracking` returning `POSITION_NOT_FOUND` (when the leg doesn't exist) or `SIZE_EXCEEDS_POSITION { positionSize, requestedSizeDelta }` (when `sizeDelta > pos.size`). Both also reject `size`/`collateral`/`sizeDelta`/`collateralDelta` strings that don't parse as positive `BigInt` with a clean `INVALID_ARGUMENT`. Pre-flight read failures fall through to the original `cast send` so a transient RPC blip can't block legitimate writes.
  - **`get_oracle_assets` slim summary + compact mode.** Response now leads with `summary: { symbols: [...], activeSymbols: [...], symbolToAssetId: {...} }` (~600 bytes at 26 assets), and a new `compact: z.boolean().optional()` input flag returns ONLY `{count, summary}` (~1.5 KB at 26 assets) so the LLM can fit a full "is X already wired?" check inside the 6000-char tool-response budget. Agent prompts updated: `agents/mining-manager.md` step 4 + step 6.d and `agents/quality-matrix-manager.md` step 4 + step 7.d now (a) tell the LLM to consult `summary.symbols` for the wired-set check and (b) document the `ALREADY_WIRED` recovery path — on `error_code: "ALREADY_WIRED"` drop wire_asset and reuse the returned `assetId` directly, do NOT re-call wire_asset for the same symbol in the same run.
  - **Atlas-ml Yahoo-resolution band-aid** in [apps/mcps/atlas-ml/index.js](apps/mcps/atlas-ml/index.js). New `symbolResolvesOnYahoo(symbol)` helper hits Yahoo's chart-meta endpoint (`https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/<symbol>?interval=1d&range=1d`) directly via `fetch` (no new dep — atlas-ml stays a thin Atlas wrapper), with a 15-min in-memory cache and a 4 s timeout. New `filterPicksByYahooResolution(picks, label)` runs every pick through it in parallel and drops non-resolvers, logging `[atlas-ml] Dropped non-resolving pick: <ticker>/<exchange> -> <yahooSymbol>` to stderr so CI captures the drops. Wired into `get_ml_top_picks`, `get_ml_basket`, `get_ml_thesis`, and `get_ml_model_info.topPredictions`. Gated behind `ATLAS_ML_VERIFY_YAHOO != "0"` (default on); env knobs `ATLAS_ML_VERIFY_TIMEOUT_MS` / `ATLAS_ML_VERIFY_TTL_MS` cover offline tests.
  - **Cross-repo feature request** at `../atlas-prototype/feature-requests/lse-ticker-yahoo-resolution.md` (after `git -C ../atlas-prototype pull --ff-only`, fast-forwarded `852cb4c..207f59d`) describing the `0R2O.L` failure, the one-line drop in [`src/atlas/financials/ticker_source_list.py#L81`](../atlas-prototype/src/atlas/financials/ticker_source_list.py) (`'0R2O'` is duplicate coverage of NYSE `FCX` already in `US_TICKERS`), and the durable fix — universe-build Yahoo pre-validation across LSE/ASX/TSX/TSXV/CSE/JSE with a 24h disk cache and duplicate-coverage suppression. Atlas repo is left unstaged for human review per the standing "do not push or PR cross-repo" policy.
  - **Tests / verification.** New `apps/mcps/vault-manager/address-validation.test.mjs` (17 cases pinning the 28-byte / 16-byte / missing-0x / non-hex rejection paths against the exact 2026-05-22 incident shape), `apps/mcps/vault-manager/revert-decoder.test.mjs` (12 cases including a `cast sig`-parity test that fails CI if any selector in `KNOWN_ERRORS` drifts from the contract's keccak), `apps/mcps/vault-manager/wire-asset-idempotency.test.mjs` (4 cases pinning the `ALREADY_WIRED` payload contract that the agent prompts depend on, plus source-text regressions confirming the `compact` mode + pre-flight wiring stay in `index.js`), and `scripts/agent-runner-vault-pin.test.mjs` (9 cases pinning that the helper covers exactly the five vault-bound write tools, overrides hallucinated / missing vaults, no-ops on read tools / non-vault tools / first-run agents, tolerates non-object args, and is case-insensitive against the canonical vault). Full suite: 181 tests pass. End-to-end smoke against Sepolia confirmed `wire_asset(CRML)` returns `ALREADY_WIRED` with the canonical assetId — exactly the recovery path the updated prompts now reference.

- [2026-05-23 03:46 UTC+05:30] **Top-picks chips on the Vault Thesis card (`apps/web/src/components/baskets/vault-thesis-card.tsx`) now render the on-chain ticker (e.g. `AHR.V`) instead of a truncated bytes32 asset id (`0x7557d8b4…a4ddd1f6`), and each chip outlinks to Yahoo Finance.** Real production agent metadata (e.g. `apps/web/public/agent-metadata/0x4dcd435461e27f8bfb580d216b8d69490023a0ba.json`) stores `recentActions[].params.assetId` as keccak-style bytes32, so `formatAssetId` correctly fell back to its hex stub — the Yahoo-Finance-gating heuristic in the chip then rejected the label and the user saw a non-linked address. Fix mirrors the same pattern `apps/web/src/components/baskets/positions-table.tsx` already uses for the open-positions table: `VaultThesisCard` now accepts an optional `assetMetaMap` prop and resolves each pick via `useOracleAssetMetaMap` (from `apps/web/src/hooks/useOracle.ts`), which keys by lowercased bytes32 asset id and returns the on-chain `assetSymbols(id)` ticker (or the mapped ERC20 `symbol()` fallback). A new `resolveTickerForAsset(assetId, assetMetaMap)` helper centralizes the precedence — prefer the on-chain `name` from the meta map, then `formatAssetId` if it decoded to printable ASCII, otherwise leave `yfinanceSymbol` null so the chip renders as a non-link. `apps/web/src/app/baskets/[address]/page.tsx` calls `useOracleAssetMetaMap()` inside `AiActivitySection` (the inner component that actually renders `VaultThesisCard`) and forwards `data` as `assetMetaMap` to the card. Tests in `apps/web/src/components/baskets/vault-thesis-card.test.ts` gain two cases pinning the new behavior (hashed asset id + map → ticker chip + Yahoo Finance anchor; hashed asset id without map → chip with no link), and the existing 7 cases still pass because they encode tickers as ASCII bytes which `formatAssetId` decodes directly.

- [2026-05-23 03:40 UTC+05:30] **`npm run deploy:local` (and the other `*:local` forge-script flows) no longer fail with `Error: No known Etherscan API URL for chain 'anvil-hardhat'` — supersedes and corrects the 03:19 entry below, which inverted the cause.** Actual upstream behaviour (foundry-rs/foundry#11351, fix in #11356; v1.3 backport #11444 merged 2025-08-29): Foundry instantiates an Etherscan trace identifier whenever `ETHERSCAN_API_KEY` is present in the *process environment* (any value — empty or not) and the active chain has no matching `[etherscan].<alias>` entry. For Anvil (chain id 31337 / `anvil-hardhat`) there is no public Etherscan URL, so the chain-id auto-derive returns `None` and forge bails out before broadcasting. The prior fix in commit `ee7ba43` inlined `ETHERSCAN_API_KEY=local-not-required` (plus the `ARBISCAN_API_KEY` / `SNOWTRACE_API_KEY` pair) on `deploy:local` / `sync:local` / `submit-sync:local` / `seed:local` in [package.json](package.json), the shared [scripts/local/redeploy.sh](scripts/local/redeploy.sh), and the workflow `env:` block in [.github/workflows/test.yml](.github/workflows/test.yml) (lines 15–16) — but setting these env vars is what *triggers* the identifier path, not what silences it, so the workaround was load-bearing in the wrong direction (it only seemed to work on CI runs that happened to be on a Foundry version with the #11444 "do not throw" relaxation, and broke once a dev shell exercised any non-relaxed code path). Canonical fix is the workaround documented by Foundry / Etherscan / Tenderly: add an explicit `local = { key = "anvil-noop", chain = 31337, url = "http://127.0.0.1:8545/verify" }` entry to the `[etherscan]` block in [foundry.toml](foundry.toml) so the chain-31337 lookup returns a hit on every forge version (including v1.3.x prior to the backport). The placeholder `url` is never contacted because none of the `*:local` scripts pass `--verify`. With the chain entry in place, the `ETHERSCAN_API_KEY=...` prefixes have been removed from all four `*:local` npm scripts and the `export ETHERSCAN_API_KEY=...` block at the top of `scripts/local/redeploy.sh` has been dropped; the workflow-level `ETHERSCAN_API_KEY: ci-not-required` / `ARBISCAN_API_KEY: ci-not-required` entries in `.github/workflows/test.yml` have also been removed (no longer needed once the chain mapping exists). Production / testnet flows (`deploy:sepolia`, `deploy:fuji`, `seed:sepolia`, `update-prices:*`) and the `[etherscan].{sepolia,arbitrum,fuji}` entries are untouched — they still read `${ETHERSCAN_API_KEY}` / `${ARBISCAN_API_KEY}` / `${SNOWTRACE_API_KEY}` from the developer's (or CI's) shell when `--verify` is run against real chains. The README paragraph at `README.md` line 619 that referenced the now-removed CI workaround was rewritten to describe the `[etherscan].local` chain-mapping fix instead. Verified locally: `forge config --root .` reports `[etherscan.local] chain = "anvil-hardhat" url = "http://127.0.0.1:8545/verify" key = "anvil-noop"` so Foundry normalizes the entry to the previously-failing chain alias.

- [2026-05-23 03:32 UTC+05:30] **`forge fmt --check` (CI `Check Solidity formatting` step) is green again after the previous "auto-fix by running forge fmt from the repo root" landed in commit `ee7ba43` but didn't actually unblock the lint.** Root cause was a forge-version mismatch between local and CI: developer machines are on `forge 1.5.1-stable` (Foundry's default for `foundryup` since late 2025), while the three workflow files pinned `FOUNDRY_VERSION: v1.3.1` (introduced in [.github/workflows/test.yml](.github/workflows/test.yml#L14), [.github/workflows/vault-agent.yml](.github/workflows/vault-agent.yml#L51), [.github/workflows/update-prices.yml](.github/workflows/update-prices.yml#L26)). The fmt rule set tightened between v1.3.1 and v1.5.1 (1.3 wraps long member-call chains differently, breaks tuple-with-comment destructurings onto multiple lines, expands single-line inline assembly blocks, and no longer collapses single-arg parameter blocks with comment placeholders into one line). Running `forge fmt` locally with v1.5.1 was therefore a no-op against files that v1.3.1 would still reject, so `git status` stayed clean while CI's `forge fmt --check` continued to flag the same 13 files — `src/perp/VaultAccounting.sol`, `script/Deploy.s.sol`, `script/DeploySpoke.s.sol`, `script/SeedTestnet.s.sol`, `script/MigrateVaultAccounting.s.sol`, plus the vendored GMX 0.6.12 mirror under `src/gmx/{core/Router.sol,core/BasePositionManager.sol,core/ShortsTracker.sol,core/VaultUtils.sol,core/PositionUtils.sol,core/SimplePriceFeed.sol,core/interfaces/IVaultPriceFeed.sol,libraries/utils/Address.sol,peripherals/Reader.sol}`. Fix bumps `FOUNDRY_VERSION: v1.3.1` → `v1.5.1` in all three workflow files so CI runs the same fmt rule set as local; no `.sol` source changes are required because the working tree already satisfies v1.5.1 fmt. Toolchain alignment also unblocks future use of v1.4/v1.5-only forge features in scripts and tests. `forge build --root /Users/reuben/Desktop/minestarters/code/snx-prototype` continues to pass on v1.5.1 (the cache reports `No files changed, compilation skipped` since fmt is byte-only and Solc input hashing is invariant under whitespace; the only remaining notices are pre-existing `forge-lint` warnings — `unsafe-typecast` on `src/perp/VaultAccounting.sol:627` and ERC20 transfer-return-value notices in test fixtures — none of which are blocking lints). Vendored GMX files are deliberately left byte-identical to the upstream 0.6.12 layout. The forge build cache key in [.github/actions/setup-snx/action.yml](.github/actions/setup-snx/action.yml#L133-L135) already mixes `${{ env.FOUNDRY_VERSION }}` so the v1.3.1 cache entries naturally roll off; the v1.5.1 key generates fresh artifacts on the next run.

- [2026-05-23 03:19 UTC+05:30] **`npm run deploy:local` (and the other `*:local` forge-script flows) no longer fail on a clean dev shell with `Error: No known Etherscan API URL for chain 'anvil-hardhat'`.** Foundry 1.5.1 + `forge script --broadcast -vvv` initializes the Etherscan trace identifier and walks every entry of `[etherscan]` in [foundry.toml](foundry.toml) regardless of which chain is being broadcast to. Because the active chain is 31337 (`anvil-hardhat`), and `${ETHERSCAN_API_KEY}` / `${ARBISCAN_API_KEY}` / `${SNOWTRACE_API_KEY}` resolve to empty strings on a developer shell that hasn't exported real explorer keys, forge takes the unknown-chain branch and bails out (regression class tracked in foundry-rs/foundry#11351 / #11444 — when the API key is non-empty, forge tolerates the unknown-chain case; when it's empty, it errors). CI was already immune because [.github/workflows/test.yml](.github/workflows/test.yml) sets `ETHERSCAN_API_KEY: ci-not-required` and `ARBISCAN_API_KEY: ci-not-required` at the workflow `env:` level (lines 15–16), and the README explicitly documents that workaround at line 619 ("placeholder explorer API key env vars for non-verify local broadcast steps in CI"). The local entry points had no equivalent. Fix mirrors CI by inlining `ETHERSCAN_API_KEY=local-not-required ARBISCAN_API_KEY=local-not-required SNOWTRACE_API_KEY=local-not-required` on every npm script that broadcasts against the local Anvil chain — `deploy:local`, `sync:local`, `submit-sync:local`, `seed:local` in [package.json](package.json) — plus the shared shell helper [scripts/local/redeploy.sh](scripts/local/redeploy.sh) (which `npm run redeploy:local` and `npm run local:up` both flow through), where the placeholders are exported with `${VAR:-local-not-required}` defaults so a developer who *has* exported a real key still wins. Production / testnet flows (`deploy:sepolia`, `deploy:fuji`, `seed:sepolia`, `update-prices:*`) are deliberately untouched — they target real chains and the developer is expected to set real explorer keys for verification. The `[etherscan]` block in `foundry.toml` is also left alone so `--verify` against Sepolia / Fuji keeps working when the real keys are set.

- [2026-05-23 02:55 UTC+05:30] **CI test job (`forge test`) is green again after `test/DeploySpokeScripts.t.sol::test_bootstrap_creates_and_funds_spoke_basket_and_allows_deposit` started reverting with `ERC20InsufficientBalance(<harness>, 0, 100_000e6)` on every CI run.** Root cause was a mismatch between the production and test execution model of `DeploySpoke._maybeBootstrapSpokeBasket`: in `run()` the function executes under `vm.startBroadcast(deployerPrivateKey)`, which pins `msg.sender` for every external call (`MockUSDC.mint`, `MockUSDC.approve`, `BasketVault.topUpReserve`'s inner `transferFrom`) to `ctx.deployer`, so the mint→approve→topUp triangle balances. Commit `312e483` removed the `vm.startPrank(ctx.deployer)` wrapper because Forge ≥1.5 rejects `startPrank` while a broadcast is active and the broadcast alone was sufficient in production — but the test harness path has no active broadcast, so the script's external calls flowed from the harness contract itself while `mint(ctx.deployer, …)` minted to the test contract, leaving the harness with 0 USDC at `topUpReserve` time (forge trace: `MockUSDC::transferFrom(DeploySpokeHarness, BasketVault, 1e11)` → `ERC20InsufficientBalance(DeploySpokeHarness, 0, 1e11)`). Fix is test-only: `test_bootstrap_creates_and_funds_spoke_basket_and_allows_deposit` now sets `ctx.deployer = address(harness)` so the mint recipient matches the actual `msg.sender` of the subsequent external calls. Comment in the test pins this invariant for future readers. Production `DeploySpoke.s.sol` is unchanged (no prank reintroduced; broadcast pinning continues to be the single source of truth for `msg.sender` in the `run()` flow). Verified locally: full `forge test` suite is `Ran 238 tests across 25 suites. Failures: 0`.
- [2026-05-23 02:55 UTC+05:30] **`forge fmt --check` (CI lint job) is green again.** Two files had drifted past the 120-char line limit and would fail the `Check Solidity formatting` step in `.github/workflows/test.yml`: `test/Integration.t.sol:583` (long `assertApproxEqAbs` call) and `script/MigrateVaultAccounting.s.sol:176` (single-line three-arg function signature with an in-line `/* usdc */` comment). Auto-fixed by running `forge fmt` from the repo root; only whitespace/wrap changes, no semantic diff. Confirmed by re-running `forge fmt --check` (exit 0).

- [2026-05-23 02:19 UTC+05:30] **Quality-matrix-manager agent recovered from a 30+ hour silent outage and the runner now forces a wire/track/open roll when the matrix rotates onto unwired picks.** Three interlocking bugs were keeping the `Minestarters Quality Matrix` vault (`0xbd7e…1f3`) at `positionCount = 0` despite $1,741.25 sitting deposited in the perp module since 2026-05-22:
  - **State file repair.** `agents/memory/quality-matrix-manager/state.json` had been corrupted by `vault-agent[bot]` commit `7e25566` into invalid JSON (a complete object closed at line 9, then orphan `"thesis"` + `"lastThesisUpdate"` keys + a second `}` appended outside the object). Every subsequent scheduled run hit `JSON.parse` in `readState` ([scripts/agent-runner.mjs](scripts/agent-runner.mjs) `readState` at L431-L434) and crashed before producing a commit, which is why no `vault-agent[bot]` commits exist for ~30 hours despite the hourly cron. File rewritten to a valid single object with `lastRunAt: 2026-05-22T20:14:24.114Z` and the orphan keys merged into the top object.
  - **CI artifact-merge corruption (root cause of the state-file Frankenstein).** Each matrix job in [.github/workflows/vault-agent.yml](.github/workflows/vault-agent.yml) was uploading the full `agents/memory/` tree, so the two artifacts ended up containing the same per-agent files. When the `commit-results` job then ran `actions/download-artifact@v4` with `merge-multiple: true`, tar extraction overlaid the SHORTER NEW file onto the LONGER OLD file at the same path without truncating, gluing the tail of the old file onto the head of the new file. Two-layer fix: (a) the upload step now scopes its path to `agents/memory/${{ matrix.agent }}/` so the two artifacts have non-overlapping per-agent subdirs (`apps/web/public/agent-metadata/` stays at the directory level because each agent writes a per-vault `<vault>.json` and vault addresses are unique per agent); (b) belt-and-braces, a new `Clean agent-managed files before artifact extraction` step in `commit-results` runs `rm -rf agents/memory/*/` plus a `find` for `apps/web/public/agent-metadata/0x*.json` before the download step, so even a future regression to "upload the whole tree" can't leave stale bytes to merge with. The two `README.md` files in those dirs are deliberately preserved.
  - **Runner policy enforcement gap (root cause of the missed opens).** The pre-existing `needsAllocation`/`needsEntry` ladder in [scripts/agent-runner.mjs](scripts/agent-runner.mjs) only forces position opens when `eligibleAssets.length > 0`, and `getEligibleQualityScoreAssets` / `getEligibleMlScoreAssets` only return picks that are BOTH above the score gate AND already on the vault's tracked-asset list. When the matrix rotated on 2026-05-22 onto names like `GRSL.V` / `A2GC.V` (not yet wired to the oracle, not yet tracked), `eligibleAssets` was empty, `needsEntry` silently never fired, and the LLM walked off after `allocate_to_perp` with no opens. Added new `getActionablePicks({ policy, picks })` helper that returns score-passing picks regardless of tracked status, plus a `needsRoll` enforcement branch that fires when `opensExecuted === 0 && eligibleAssets.length === 0 && actionablePicks.length > 0`. The new directive listing (a) the current top-N symbols, (b) the subset NOT yet present in `get_oracle_assets` (which must be `yfinance_quote`'d then `wire_asset`'d in that order), (c) a `set_vault_assets` roll to the current top-N capped at `maxTrackedAssets`, and (d) the `open_position` count cap derived from `maxNewPositionsPerRun`. Bumps `policyRuntime.enforcementRounds` like the existing branches so it can fire up to 8 times.
  - **Zero-amount allocate guard (bonus).** Run 1 of the same agent on 2026-05-21 burned the entire turn budget because the LLM called `allocate_to_perp` with `amount: "0"` against a freshly created (empty) vault, then re-proposed it on retry until `cast send` reverted with `Amount required`. The runner now intercepts these calls pre-`cast send`: when `allocate_to_perp` is proposed with `amount === "0"` (or numeric `0`) while `allocationAmountRaw > 0n`, the runner emits a Policy violation directive with the correct amount and the active vault address, then `continue`s the loop so the LLM retries with the right value instead of paying for a guaranteed revert.
  - **Diagnostics.** `runSummary.policyDiagnostics` gains `actionablePickSymbols: string[]` and `needsRollTriggered: boolean` so the next operator looking at a run log can see at a glance whether the matrix rotation was the trigger. Tests: 5 new cases in `scripts/agent-runner-policy.test.mjs` pin `getActionablePicks` (quality-mode + ml-mode score gating, the `maxTrackedAssets` cap, the "no picks" / "non-score entry mode" / "null picks" early returns, and the de-dup-by-yahooSymbol behavior so a pick appearing twice in the response doesn't double-count against the cap).

- [2026-05-23 02:12 UTC+05:30] **Quality-matrix basket regained its `AI` flag on `/baskets` (and the AI Operator badge / Atlas-quality chip / Vault-Thesis card on `/baskets/0xbd7e…1f3`).** The static metadata file `apps/web/public/agent-metadata/0xbd7ea7e23ae07f0dd65e0738babf8864fdd741f3.json` had been corrupted in vault-agent[bot] commit `7e25566` — a complete JSON object closed at line 80, then 21 stray lines (a partial duplicate of the recent-actions tail starting mid-string with `entName": "quality-matrix-manager",`) were appended after the closing `}`. `JSON.parse` threw, `useAgentMetadata` returned `null`, and every UI surface gated on `agentMeta?.isAiManaged` silently disappeared (basket card chip in `apps/web/src/components/baskets/basket-card.tsx` lines 95–100, plus `AiOperatorBadge`, `signalSource === "atlas-ml"` chip, and `AiActivitySection` / Vault-Thesis card on the detail page). Repair restores the file to the byte-identical clean state from commit `76e010a`. Defensive hardening: `apps/web/src/hooks/useAgentMetadata.ts` now retries with a balanced-brace scanner (`extractFirstJsonObject`) that respects strings/escapes, so a future bot run that re-introduces trailing garbage still parses the leading object instead of dropping the AI flag from the UI.

- [2026-05-23 02:15 UTC+05:30] **Leverage metric in the basket detail metrics strip no longer shows astronomically long values.** The tile was dividing 1e30-scaled open interest by 6-decimal USDC deposited capital without the `1e24` scale adjustment, then calling `Number#toFixed(2)`, which overflowed the metric cell despite CSS truncation. New `formatLeverageRatio` in `apps/web/src/lib/format.ts` computes leverage in bigint (`openInterest / depositedCapital / 1e24`) and renders compact values like `2.5x` or `1,234.56x`; both `apps/web/src/app/baskets/[address]/page.tsx` and `apps/web/src/app/admin/baskets/[address]/page.tsx` use it, and the incorrect `leverageRatio` number was removed from `useBasketDashboardData`.

- [2026-05-23 01:00 UTC+05:30] **Basket deposit no longer crashes with "Maximum update depth exceeded" once the user clicks `Confirm deposit`.** The mirror effect in `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-confirm-modal.tsx` (the one that copies per-chain progress into the global transaction store so `TransactionDock` keeps showing the deposit after the modal is dismissed) listed the entire `useOptionalTransactionStatus()` return value in its dep array. That combined context value is rebuilt via `useMemo([..., state.records])` in `apps/web/src/providers/TransactionStatusProvider.tsx` on every dispatch, and the `update-child` branch of `txStoreReducer` always returns a fresh `records` array even when the patch is a no-op. The effect itself dispatches `updateChild` per chain, so each dispatch produced a new `txStatus` reference, which re-fired the effect, which dispatched again, looping until React tripped its depth guard and unmounted the Radix `Dialog` subtree (which is why the stack trace surfaced inside `usePresence` / `compose-refs` rather than at the actual dispatch site). The condition only triggered once `parentTxIdRef.current` was set and `chainStatuses.length > 0`, which matches the "I get this when I try to deposit" reproduction. Fix splits the provider into two contexts so action-only consumers don't subscribe to record updates: `TransactionStateContext` (`records`, `activeTxs`, `inFlightCount`, `latestTx`) and `TransactionActionsContext` (`startTx`, `updateTx`, `updateChild`, `markSubmitted`, `completeTx`, `failTx`, `dismissTx`, `clearCompleted`, `getExplorerUrl`). All actions are wrapped in `useCallback` and the actions context value is memoized over those stable callback refs, so the returned reference does not change when records mutate and is therefore safe to put in `useEffect` dep arrays. New hooks `useTransactionState` / `useOptionalTransactionState` and `useTransactionActions` / `useOptionalTransactionActions`; the legacy `useTransactionStatus` / `useOptionalTransactionStatus` hooks remain for backwards compatibility but their TSDoc now warns that they return a fresh object reference on every render and must not be placed in dep arrays. Consumers migrated: the deposit confirm modal's mirror effect and `handleConfirm` now read `useOptionalTransactionActions()`; `apps/web/src/components/transactions/transaction-dock.tsx` splits between `useOptionalTransactionState()` (for records + `inFlightCount`) and `useOptionalTransactionActions()` (for `dismissTx`, `clearCompleted`, `getExplorerUrl`), with its auto-dismiss effect deps tightened from `[tx]` to `[records, dismissTx]`; the internal `useTrackedTx` (used by every wagmi write in `apps/web/src/hooks/useBasketVault.ts`) and `useTxByHash` were refactored to use the split hooks transparently. Secondary hardening: `DepositRedeemPanel.handleDepositSuccess` (the `onSuccess` callback passed into the modal) is now wrapped in `useCallback([], ...)` so the modal's phase-transition effect doesn't re-fire on every parent render. Test updates: `apps/web/src/providers/TransactionStatusProvider.test.ts` gains an API-surface test pinning the new exports and a reducer test documenting that `update-child` always produces a new `records` array (the loop-trigger condition, kept as a deliberate trade-off so the split contexts stay the load-bearing fix); `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-confirm-modal.test.ts` mock surface adds `useOptionalTransactionActions` / `useOptionalTransactionState`.

- [2026-05-23 00:55 UTC+05:30] **`/chains` page no longer shows duplicate Sepolia + Fuji rows.** The Envio handler `StateRelay.StateUpdated` was keying `ChainPoolState` by `${chainId}-${chainSelector}` while reading the *global* routing table (every chain's selector) on every event, so each `chainSelector` was being written once per relay that observed it — producing four rows for a Sepolia↔Fuji deployment (`11155111-sepoliaSelector`, `11155111-fujiSelector`, `43113-sepoliaSelector`, `43113-fujiSelector`) which the front-end faithfully rendered as "2 Sepolia + 2 Fuji". Two-layer fix: (a) `apps/envio/src/EventHandlers.ts::chainPoolId` now keys solely by `chainSelector` (last-writer-wins; both relays publish the same logical routing table so values converge), and the entity's `chainId` field now records which relay last reported the value. `apps/envio/schema.graphql` and `apps/envio/README.md` document `ChainPoolState` as the explicit exception to the chain-scoped `<chainId>-...` ID convention. (b) `apps/web/src/hooks/usePoolReserveRegistry.ts::transformChainPoolStates` adds defense-in-depth dedupe by grouping raw rows by `chainSelector` and keeping the freshest by `updatedAt` before the existing weight/staleness derivation — this keeps the UI clean while the indexer is being redeployed/replayed and shields against any future regression that re-introduces duplicates. New `apps/web/src/hooks/usePoolReserveRegistry.test.ts` case feeds two same-selector rows with different `updatedAt` and asserts the output collapses to one row using the freshest snapshot. **Operator note:** Envio Cloud auto-deploys on push to `main` (per `AGENT_DEPLOYMENT_MEMORY.md` `indexflow-prototype-3`); the redeploy will replay events from genesis and replace existing duplicate rows with the new selector-only IDs. Locally, restart `npm run --prefix apps/envio dev:local` if running.

### Changed

- [2026-05-23 02:33 UTC+05:30] **Replaced the `APY (all)` metric tile on the basket detail page with a `PnL` (since inception) tile sourced from the same Envio `Basket` entity the basket-list card reads, so the two views display the exact same number.** The earlier in-development iterations of `APY (all)` (introduced 02:05 UTC+05:30 as a `createdAt`-anchored annualisation, then reworked 02:18 UTC+05:30 to read the earliest indexed `BasketSnapshot`) kept rendering as `--` for vaults whose snapshot baseline / inception data hadn't backfilled — for example `Minestarters ML Picks` (`0xB282…B3F6`) showed `--` on the detail page even though the basket-list card next to it surfaced a real PnL value. A first attempt at this fix wired the detail tile to `basketInfo.sharePrice` (the live `PerpReader.getBasketInfo` RPC read), but indexer-vs-RPC drift could still make the two views disagree on the same vault. Final fix sources the share price for the PnL tile from `useBasketDetailQuery` (the same Envio `Basket` entity the list card reads via `useBasketsOverviewQuery` / `useMultiChainBaskets`), exposed as `subgraphSharePrice` on `useBasketDashboardData`. The detail metric is `formatPnLPct(subgraphSharePrice)` + `computePnLPctBps(subgraphSharePrice)` — bytewise identical input to the list card's `formatPnLPct(info.sharePrice)`, so the rendered string also matches. Color tone follows the same `text-app-success` / `text-app-danger` / `text-app-text` rules as the existing PnL tiles via `MetricCell.{pnl, sign}`. Test ID is `metric-pnl-pct` (was `metric-apy-all-time`). Wiring:
  - **`apps/web/src/hooks/useBasketDashboardData.ts`**: expose `subgraphSharePrice = basketDetail.data?.basket?.sharePrice ?? null` (was already fetched via the existing `useBasketDetailQuery` call, so no new network request); drop `apyAllTime`, `inceptionSec`, the `computeAllTimeApy` import, the `PRICE_PRECISION` import, and the snapshot-baseline computation block. Hook return shape gains `subgraphSharePrice` and otherwise regresses to the pre-02:05 surface (`apy7d` is the only APY-related field).
  - **`apps/web/src/app/baskets/[address]/page.tsx`** + **`apps/web/src/app/admin/baskets/[address]/page.tsx`**: drop the `apyAllTime` / `apyAllTimeSign` derivation, import `formatPnLPct` + `computePnLPctBps` from `@/lib/format`, read `subgraphSharePrice` from the hook, and replace the `APY (all)` `MetricCell` with the new `PnL` cell. While `subgraphSharePrice === null` (subgraph still loading or unavailable) the tile renders `--` rather than falling back to RPC — the trade-off is a slightly slower first paint on the detail page in exchange for the guarantee that the value never disagrees with the list card.
  - **`apps/web/src/lib/apy.ts`**: drop the `computeAllTimeApy` helper and the `AllTimeApyBaseline` type. `apps/web/src/lib/apy.test.ts` drops the matching 6 cases; `computeApy` / `formatApy` coverage stays intact (6 cases).
  - **`apps/web/src/lib/subgraph/envio-queries.ts::ENVIO_GET_BASKET_TREND_SNAPSHOTS`**: drop the `earliestWeekSnapshots` selection added 02:18 — there's no consumer left.
  - **`apps/web/src/hooks/subgraph/useBasketTrends.ts`**: drop the `earliest` field on `BasketTrendSeries`, the `earliestRows` parameter on `toBasketTrendSeries`, and the request-side typing for `earliestWeekSnapshots`. The `emptyBasketTrendSnapshots()` shape regresses accordingly.
  - **`apps/web/src/lib/tooltip-copy.ts`**: drop the `apyAllTime` tooltip key (no longer referenced; the new `PnL` tile reuses the existing `pnlSinceInception` copy if a future change wires a tooltip onto the metric strip).
  - **Left in place** for cleanliness: `BasketDetail.basket.createdAt` plumbing in `apps/web/src/hooks/subgraph/useBasketDetail.ts` (and the `createdAt` selection in `ENVIO_GET_BASKET_DETAIL`) — type-safe, unused, and a likely surface for a future "Created N days ago" badge / tooltip on the new PnL tile.

- [2026-05-23 02:05 UTC+05:30] **Redesigned the Vault Thesis tile on the basket detail page from a single-paragraph bordered block into a focal "strategy story" panel.** The old block in `AiActivitySection` (`apps/web/src/app/baskets/[address]/page.tsx`, lines 567–579) — a `Lightbulb` icon plus an uppercase label plus one `<p>` of `agentMeta.thesis` — was extracted into a new client component `apps/web/src/components/baskets/vault-thesis-card.tsx` with a quote-glyph hero treatment (gradient `from-app-accent/10 via-app-accent/5 to-transparent` shell, decorative `Quote` lucide icon at `text-app-accent/15`, a 4px `border-l-app-accent/70` accent rail, and an enlarged title with an "AI operator strategy" subtitle that includes the agent description). Header chips on the right surface `signalSource` (Atlas ML / Atlas Quality, mapped via `SIGNAL_SOURCE_LABEL`), `entryMode` (humanized via `ENTRY_MODE_LABEL`), and "Updated …" relative time computed from `lastRunAt` via the existing `formatRelativeTime` helper. Body changes:
  - **Markdown rendering** via `react-markdown` + `remark-gfm` (already in `apps/web/package.json`) with a custom `components` map that styles `p`, `strong`, `em`, `ul`/`ol`/`li`, `code`, `a`, and demotes `h1`/`h2`/`h3` to small uppercase tracking labels. The model-emitted thesis often arrives with markdown headings (e.g. `## Thesis`) which previously rendered as literal text in the prior plain-`<p>` implementation.
  - **Ticker auto-highlighting with Yahoo Finance outlinks.** New exported `highlightTickers(text)` helper splits string children on a deliberately narrow regex `(\b[A-Z0-9]{1,6}\.[A-Z]{1,3}\b|\(\s*[A-Z][A-Z0-9.\-]{1,7}\s*\))` so suffix-exchange tickers like `AHR.V` / `GTWO.TO` / `0KXS.L` and parenthesized tickers like `(CRML)` get wrapped in mono accent chips, while common all-caps words ("Atlas ML", "USDC", "AI") are left as prose. Each chip is now an `<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">` pointing at `yahooFinanceQuoteUrl(symbol)` (from `apps/web/src/lib/yahoo-finance.ts`, the same helper already used by `positions-table.tsx` / `asset-price-panel.tsx` / `admin/oracle/page.tsx`); a small `tickerToYfinanceSymbol(token)` helper strips the wrapping parens off `(CRML)`-style matches before they hit the URL builder so `https://finance.yahoo.com/quote/CRML/` resolves correctly while the displayed text keeps the parens. `aria-label="View {symbol} on Yahoo Finance"` is set on every anchor for screen readers. The helper short-circuits on no-match input and returns the original string. `mapStringChildren` plumbs it through every text-bearing markdown component.
  - **Top-picks chip rail** (data-testid `vault-thesis-top-picks`) derived from `recentActions.filter(a => a.runId === latestRun.runId && a.tool === "open_position")`. Each unique `(assetId, isLong)` becomes a chip with `TrendingUp` / `TrendingDown` icon, `Long` / `Short` label, and the human-readable asset id. Tone classes come from the existing `getToneChipClass` in `apps/web/src/lib/agent-action-meta.ts` so `text-app-success` / `text-app-danger` keep the long/short coloring consistent with the "Show all decisions" panel. Each chip wraps in a Yahoo Finance anchor when the decoded asset id is a real ticker (gated on `!label.startsWith("0x")`, the same heuristic used by the positions table). Older-run actions and non-position tools are filtered out.
  - **Read-more / Read less toggle** (data-testid `vault-thesis-toggle`) uses Tailwind core `line-clamp-5` (no plugin needed) and is gated on `shouldClampThesis(text)` (`length > 360 || /\n\n/`). Short single-paragraph theses don't render the toggle at all.
  - **Graceful fallback when `thesis` is null.** Vaults like the quality-matrix-manager (`0xbd7e…`) currently publish `thesis: null` because the runner only fills the field when the LLM produces a `## Thesis` section. The card now renders a "Thesis pending …" italic preface plus the markdown-rendered `latestRun.summary` so the panel is never empty mid-bootstrap. When BOTH the thesis and the latest-run summary are missing, the card falls through to an `Awaiting first run …` empty state (data-testid `vault-thesis-empty`).
  - The component is wired into `AiActivitySection` unconditionally (the section itself only renders for AI-managed vaults), replacing the old `agentMeta.thesis && (...)` guard. Unused `Lightbulb` import was removed from the page; `InfoTooltipLazy` stays since the AI Operator header still uses it.
  - Tests: new `apps/web/src/components/baskets/vault-thesis-card.test.ts` (7 cases) covers the `highlightTickers` helper (suffix + parenthesized matches, no-match passthrough), the rendered ticker chips, signal-source / entry-mode / Updated chips, the run-scoped top-picks rail (older runs filtered), the latest-run-summary fallback, the empty state, and the Read more visibility predicate.
  - Docs sync: `apps/web/src/lib/tooltip-copy.ts::vaultThesis` rewritten to describe the chips, top-picks rail, fallback, and Read more toggle. `docs/AGENTS_FRAMEWORK.md` "Web-facing metadata schema" table updated — `thesis` row now points at `apps/web/src/components/baskets/vault-thesis-card.tsx` and documents the fallback to `latestRun.summary`; `latestRun` row mentions its dual role as both the "Latest" pill driver in the decisions panel and the thesis-card fallback body; `recentActions[]` row notes the top-picks rail derivation. `apps/web/src/lib/wiki.ts` does not cover this surface so no in-app wiki update was needed.

- [2026-05-23 02:00 UTC+05:30] **`/baskets` cards now show PnL (% since inception) and Assets count instead of Share price and Perp exposure, and the perp-only Allocated bar was merged into a single Idle vs Allocated composition bar.** The 2-col grid in `apps/web/src/components/baskets/basket-card.tsx` was repurposed: the left tile becomes `PnL` (label-only icon `sharePrice`, value from new helper `formatPnLPct(sharePrice)` in `apps/web/src/lib/format.ts`, color-coded via `text-app-success / text-app-danger / text-app-muted` based on sign — matches the existing APY block), and the right tile becomes `Assets` (icon `assets` / Layers3, value `assetCount.toLocaleString()`). The thin bar below is no longer perp-only: it's a single horizontal bar split between `idlePct = usdcBalance / TVL` and `perpPct = 100 - idlePct`, with a `Composition` label and `X% idle · Y% allocated` reading on the right. New `pnlSinceInception` tooltip copy added to `apps/web/src/lib/tooltip-copy.ts`; the Assets tile reuses the existing `tableAssets` tooltip. The card's `perpBlendBps` prop, the page-side `computeBlendedComposition` call, the `useVaultStateBatch` / `useAllBaskets` `openInterestByVault` derivation, and the unused `Address` import are removed from `apps/web/src/app/baskets/page.tsx`. The sort segmented control's `Price` option is renamed to `PnL` (sort key `"price"` → `"pnl"`); underlying sort is still by `sharePrice`, since PnL is monotonic in share price for unit-priced baskets. Test surface: `apps/web/src/components/baskets/basket-card.test.ts` now asserts `PnL` / `+25.00%` / `Assets` / asset count / `Composition` / `100% idle` / `0% allocated` and explicitly asserts the old `Share price` and `Perp exposure` labels are gone; the zero-PnL case verifies `0.00%` renders without a `+` or `-` sign.

- [2026-05-23 01:25 UTC+05:30] **Deposit confirm modal can now be minimized while transactions are in-flight, and the entire deposit UI scales down to phone viewports.** Previously the modal hard-blocked all close gestures while any chain was mid-execution (the wrapper's `handleOpenChange` short-circuited with `[DepositConfirmModal] Prevented close during execution`) even though the body text told the user they could close it — because the per-chain hook (`useParallelChainDeposits`) and the global `TransactionDock` mirroring effect lived inside `DepositConfirmModalContent`, which Radix unmounts on close, so actually allowing the close would have silently frozen the dock at the last reported state and lost the confirmed/failed transition. Three-layer fix:
  - **Lift execution state up one mount level** in `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-confirm-modal.tsx`. The `useParallelChainDeposits()` instantiation, `parentTxIdRef`, the dock-mirroring `useEffect`, the phase state machine (`preview | executing | complete | error`), the executing -> complete/error effect, and the 160s safety-timeout all moved into the outer `DepositConfirmModal` wrapper (always mounted alongside the panel). `DepositConfirmModalContent` (only mounted while open) was reduced to preview-time computation + presentation: it still resolves vault twins via `useVaultAddressByName`, computes `splits` / `executionVaultMappings` / `needsApprovalPerChain`, polls gas via `useChainGasEstimates({ enabled: phase === "preview" })`, runs the sponsorship error dialog, and now passes the freshly-computed args up to a wrapper-supplied `onConfirm(args)` instead of holding the hook itself. The content reads `phase` and `depositState` from props; when `phase !== "preview"` it falls back to `depositState.chainStatuses` for the per-chain row data so a minimize -> maximize during execution renders against authoritative live data even if a brief react-query refetch is in flight.
  - **Minimize semantics** replace the close-blocker in `handleOpenChange`. During `phase === "executing"`, X / Esc / overlay tap and the new explicit `Minimize` button (lucide `Minimize2` icon, `data-testid="deposit-confirm-minimize"`) all just call `onOpenChange(false)` without touching the lifted state — execution and dock updates continue. In every other phase the same gesture resets the lifted state (`reset()` + `setPhase("preview")`) and closes. The misleading "Transactions will continue in the background" copy was replaced by a directional "Minimize this dialog and the transaction dock will keep showing progress — tap the dock card to bring this dialog back" helper underneath the button.
  - **Maximize from the dock** via a new `meta.onResume?: () => void` field on `TxRecord` in `apps/web/src/providers/TransactionStatusProvider.tsx`. The modal wrapper sets it when calling `startTx` (the callback is `() => onOpenChange(true)`); the dock-mirroring effect clears it via `updateTx(id, { meta: undefined })` immediately before `completeTx` on the success branch (no value in re-opening a confirmed deposit during the 4s linger), and deliberately leaves it intact on the failure branch so the user can tap a failed dock card to maximize and hit Retry. `TransactionDock` (`apps/web/src/components/transactions/transaction-dock.tsx`) gained two behaviors: when `record.meta.onResume` is present the `MiniCard`'s primary tap target calls `onResume` instead of `onExpand`, the trailing `ChevronUp` swaps to a `Maximize2` icon, and the aria-label becomes `Reopen ${record.label}`; the `ExpandedRow` body becomes a `Reopen ${record.label}` button under the same condition. Resumable cards expose `data-resumable="true"` for E2E.
  - **Panel-side input lock** in `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-redeem-panel.tsx`. The modal accepts a new optional `onInFlightChange?: (active: boolean) => void` prop that fires when its phase machine leaves / returns to `"preview"`; the panel mirrors that into a local `isDepositInFlight` flag that disables the amount Input (`disabled` attribute), hides the `Max` shortcut, and swaps the Deposit / Multi-Chain Deposit CTA label to "Resume deposit" (which still just calls `setIsDepositModalOpen(true)` — clicking it maximizes the modal without starting a new flow). Prevents the `amount` prop on the modal from drifting underneath the live execution.
  - **Mobile-friendliness pass on every deposit surface in the same edit session.** `apps/web/src/components/ui/dialog.tsx` `DialogContent` swaps `w-full max-w-lg` for `w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)] max-w-lg` (always leaves a side margin on phones, unchanged from `sm` up), `p-6` for `p-4 sm:p-6`, adds `max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain` so tall modals get internal scroll instead of overflowing the viewport, and the close X becomes a 36x36 hit target (`absolute right-3 top-3 inline-flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-md`). The deposit modal drops its hard `max-w-2xl` for `sm:max-w-2xl`, wraps the `DialogTitle` (`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3` + `break-words` on the title span), reflows `RoutingSummary` rows into a stacked layout below `sm` (`flex flex-col gap-2 ... sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between` with an inner `justify-between` for the amount / gas row), wraps `StepRow` trailing clusters via `flex-wrap` + `truncate` on the tx hash, and applies `flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end` to all three footer branches so buttons render full-width on mobile (the new executing-phase footer also uses this for the Minimize button). The deposit panel tightens to `Card p-4 sm:p-5`, adds `inputMode="decimal"` to the amount Input so iOS shows a numeric keypad, wraps the amount-row header via `flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2`, and adds `flex-wrap` to every `InlineTxStepper` phase row plus `shrink-0` on the explorer-link button so the verb / hash / View cluster wraps cleanly on narrow viewports. The dock cards bump their action-icon buttons (View / Dismiss / Retry) from `p-1` to `inline-flex h-8 w-8` so each meets a ~36px touch target without growing the row visually.
  - **Tests / docs.** `apps/web/src/providers/TransactionStatusProvider.test.ts` gains two reducer cases pinning the `meta.onResume` contract: `start` accepts the callback and `update` with `{ meta: undefined }` clears it; `complete` -> failed leaves the meta intact (the failed-row Retry path depends on this). `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-confirm-modal.test.ts` adds a smoke test confirming the wrapper accepts the new `onInFlightChange` prop without throwing. Tooltip copy in `apps/web/src/lib/tooltip-copy.ts::multiChainDepositStatus` rewritten to mention the minimize / maximize loop. `docs/INVESTOR_FLOW.md` deposit step describes the minimize affordance; `docs/CROSS_CHAIN_COORDINATION.md` "Unified Deposit Confirm Modal" status-tracking bullet now explains why the modal can be minimized without losing progress (wrapper stays mounted, content unmounts) and documents the `meta.onResume` clear-on-success / preserve-on-failure rule.

- [2026-05-23 01:10 UTC+05:30] **Basket card now distinguishes "Perp exposure" (open-interest share) from "Allocated" (capital share) so the tile percentage and the progress bar can't be confused for the same metric.** Previously the right-hand tile on each card in `apps/web/src/components/baskets/basket-card.tsx` was labelled "Perp sleeve" with its value driven by `perpBlendBps = openInterest / (spotCapital + openInterest)` from `apps/web/src/lib/blendedComposition.ts::computeBlendedComposition`, while the thin progress bar directly below was — without any label or numeric value — driven by `perpAllocated / TVL`. A vault with capital parked in the perp accounting system but no currently open positions therefore rendered a non-zero bar AND a "Perp sleeve 0%" reading, which read as a bug even though both numbers were internally correct against their formulas. Fix: (a) the tile is renamed to "Perp exposure" and wrapped in `InfoLabel tooltipKey="perpExposure"` (existing copy: "Aggregate perp sleeve exposure derived from current position notional"); (b) the unlabeled progress bar is now preceded by a caption row reading `Allocated  X%` (with `InfoLabel tooltipKey="perpAllocated"`) where `X` is `Number((perpAllocated * 100n) / tvl)` computed once and reused for both the caption and the bar width — the prior fallback string `"X% in perp"` / `"No perp allocation"` is removed since the bar carries the visualization. The same rename was applied to the two other places that displayed `perpBlendBps` next to the literal phrase "Perp sleeve": the basket subtitle on `apps/web/src/app/dashboard/page.tsx` (`Perp sleeve {formatBps(perpBlendBps)}` → `Perp exposure {formatBps(perpBlendBps)}`) and the aggregate `StatCard` `subValue` on `apps/web/src/app/admin/page.tsx`. Test surface: `apps/web/src/components/baskets/basket-card.test.ts` now asserts `Perp exposure` + `Allocated` instead of the old `Perp sleeve`. Known follow-up (deliberately out of scope, picked option "split & label"): the `/baskets` all-chains view in `apps/web/src/app/baskets/page.tsx` still computes `openInterestByVault` as an empty map when `viewMode === "all"`, so every card in that view will show `Perp exposure 0.00%` regardless of actual exposure — fix by plumbing `openInterest` through `useMultiChainBaskets` in a follow-up.

- [2026-05-23 01:05 UTC+05:30] **"Show all decisions" panel on the basket detail page now groups agent actions by run, renders icon-driven cards with per-tool tones, and surfaces a structured params summary (asset, side, size, collateral, leverage, amount, fee bps, etc.) instead of just the LLM's free-text justification.** Three layers:
  - `scripts/agent-runner.mjs::publishAgentMetadata` now attaches a `params` field to every entry in `apps/web/public/agent-metadata/<vault>.json::recentActions[]` via a new `summarizeActionParams(tool, args)` helper. The helper drops the redundant `vault` address and `justification` and only keeps human-meaningful fields per write tool (`wire_asset` → `{symbol, seedPriceUsd?}`, `create_vault` → `{name, depositFeeBps, redeemFeeBps, deployToSpokes?}`, `set_vault_assets` → `{assetIds, count}`, `allocate_to_perp` / `withdraw_from_perp` → `{amountUsdc}` raw 6dp, `open_position` → `{assetId, isLong, size, collateral}`, `close_position` → `{assetId, isLong, sizeDelta, collateralDelta}`). Unknown / read-only tools omit `params` entirely. The new field is piped through the same `redactSecretsDeep` pass as the rest of the metadata so the security posture is unchanged. Schema documented in `apps/web/public/agent-metadata/README.md`; `AgentActionParams` discriminated union added to `apps/web/src/hooks/useAgentMetadata.ts`; new `scripts/agent-runner-action-params.test.mjs` covers every supported tool, the unknown-tool no-op, and string coercion of numeric `size`/`collateral`.
  - New `apps/web/src/lib/agent-action-meta.ts` centralizes the per-tool icon/label/tone map (`create_vault` → Landmark/accent, `wire_asset` → Link2/warning, `set_vault_assets` → Layers3/accent, `allocate_to_perp` → TrendingUp/accent, `withdraw_from_perp` → TrendingDown/warning, `open_position` → PlusCircle/success-or-danger depending on `isLong`, `close_position` → MinusCircle/muted, fallback Activity/muted) plus `renderActionChips(action)` which formats `params` into UI chips using the existing `formatUSDC` / `formatUsd1e30` / `formatAssetId` / `formatBps` helpers and computes leverage as `size / (collateral * 1e24)` for open positions. Tile and chip tone classes are exported (`getToneTileClass`, `getToneChipClass`) so the same palette as `HistoryActivityMeta` in `apps/web/src/components/baskets/basket-detail-ui.tsx` is reused. `humanizeToolName` moves here so the panel and the vault history row share one implementation.
  - `AiActivitySection` in `apps/web/src/app/baskets/[address]/page.tsx` (the AI Operator card) was rewritten: the header now surfaces `signalSource` and `entryMode` (previously unused in the UI) as small pills next to the agent name; the flat `<ul>` list inside the collapsible "Show all decisions" panel is replaced by run-grouped sections built from a new `groupActionsByRun(actions)` helper. Each run renders a header with a `Run N of M` badge, relative time, `Latest` pill when its `runId` matches `latestRun.runId`, an action count, and on-chain vs off-chain count chips, plus a responsive `grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3` of action cards. Each action card has a tone-coded 40x40 icon tile, a humanized tool label, an `On-chain` / `Off-chain` pill driven by `txHash` presence, the params chip row, a `line-clamp-3` italic justification, and a `View tx · 0x1234…abcd` link with an `ExternalLink` glyph (or a muted `No on-chain effect` line when `txHash` is null). Tooltip copy in `apps/web/src/lib/tooltip-copy.ts::aiOperator` was extended to describe the new grouped view; `docs/AGENTS_FRAMEWORK.md` "Web-facing metadata schema" table now lists the optional `params` field on `recentActions[]` and points readers at the per-tool shapes in the agent-metadata README.

- [2026-05-23 00:55 UTC+05:30] **Unified deposit flow into a single `DepositConfirmModal` that bundles approve + deposit per chain and surfaces live cross-chain routing + gas estimates.** The user now enters the USDC amount once and clicks `Deposit` / `Multi-Chain Deposit`; the modal handles `approve(vault, amount)` + `deposit(amount)` sequentially on each eligible chain, so the previous separate "Approve USDC" click in the basket trade panel is gone. The old slide-up `MultiChainDepositDrawer` (`apps/web/src/components/baskets/multi-chain-deposit-drawer.tsx`) and the now-unused `useMultiChainDeposit` hook (`apps/web/src/hooks/useMultiChainDeposit.ts`) were deleted. Implementation:
  - New `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-confirm-modal.tsx` built on the existing `Dialog` primitive (`max-w-2xl`) with phases `loading | no-routing | preview | executing | complete | error` (mirrored to `data-testid="deposit-confirm-modal-phase"` for E2E). Preview shows a `RoutingBar` plus a condensed per-chain summary row (chain icon, %, USDC amount, `Fuel`-iconed gas estimate, "Approve + Deposit" vs "Deposit only" pill driven by the pre-flight allowance read). A collapsible "Routing & network details" panel (chevron toggle, auto-expands during execution) reveals per-chain `Approve USDC` and `Deposit` step rows with their individual gas units (`~123,456 gas`, marked `(est.)` when the deposit estimate fell back because the vault has no allowance yet), tx-hash badges as they confirm, per-chain native cost, and an aggregate row showing "Total network cost" summed by `nativeSymbol` (handles ETH + AVAX in the same multi-chain split). Execution view reuses the same per-chain rows with `switching | approving | depositing | success | error` status icons; the modal mirrors progress into `useOptionalTransactionStatus` so closing the dialog doesn't lose state in the global `TransactionDock`.
  - `apps/web/src/hooks/useParallelChainDeposits.ts` is now the single execution engine for all deposits — both single-chain (1 split) and multi-chain (N splits) — with three behaviour changes: (a) approve detection switched from string-sniffing the deposit error message to an explicit pre-flight `useAllowancesPerChain(owner, splits)` hook that fans out `ERC20.allowance(owner, vault)` reads via `getPublicClient(wagmiConfig, { chainId })` per chain and feeds `computeNeedsApprovalPerChain(splits, allowances) -> Record<chainId, boolean>` into the modal's preview AND `execute({ needsApprovalPerChain })`; (b) the hook now `invalidateAllowanceCache(chainId, vaultAddress)` after every successful deposit via `useQueryClient().invalidateQueries` keyed on `["allowances-per-chain", ...]` + any matching wagmi `readContract`/`simulateContract` keys for `ERC20.allowance(owner, vault)` on that chain — this fixes the bug where the panel button (and any other allowance consumers) wouldn't reflect that the approval was consumed by the last deposit until the next polling tick; (c) added a non-sponsored fallback path that resolves a wallet client via `getWalletClient(wagmiConfig, { chainId })` and switches chains via `useSwitchChain.switchChainAsync({ chainId })` before each tx, so external-wallet (non-Privy) deposits flow through the same modal instead of needing a separate code path.
  - New `apps/web/src/hooks/useChainGasEstimates.ts` returns per-chain `{ approveGas, depositGas, gasPrice, nativeCostWei, nativeCostFormatted, nativeSymbol, depositEstimateIsFallback }`. Approve gas is estimated only when `needsApproval` is true; deposit `estimateGas` is wrapped in try/catch and falls back to a `250_000n` constant (with the flag set) so the UI still has a number to display before the on-chain approval lands. Native symbol + decimals come from the wagmi chain definition (`wagmiConfig.chains.find(c => c.id === chainId)?.nativeCurrency`). React-query refresh is `30s` while the modal is in preview and pauses (`enabled: phase === "preview"`) once execution starts.
  - `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-redeem-panel.tsx` was trimmed: removed `useApproveUSDC` + `useDeposit` imports and the entire two-step approve/deposit branch from `handleSubmit`; the deposit button (single-chain or multi-chain) always opens the new modal; the `InlineTxStepper` component is now redeem-only (deposits never render the inline rail anymore); helper copy under the quote box now reads `"Approval and deposit are bundled — review routing, network costs, and per-chain steps in the next dialog."`; the panel still calls `useSimulateDeposit` for the input-side error preview but no longer blocks submission on it (the modal re-simulates per chain with chain-specific guidance).
  - Tooltip copy in `apps/web/src/lib/tooltip-copy.ts`: `approveAction` and `depositAction` rewritten to describe the bundled modal flow; `multiChainDepositStatus` + `multiChainDepositParallel` updated; new `multiChainDepositDetails` key for the expandable details panel; obsolete `multiChainDepositMinimize` key removed (the new modal doesn't minimize — the global `TransactionDock` covers that role).
  - Docs: `docs/CROSS_CHAIN_COORDINATION.md` "Frontend Deposit Splitting" section replaced the "Automated Multi-Chain Deposit Flow (Privy Users)" + "Key components" lists with a new "Unified Deposit Confirm Modal" walkthrough covering preview / expandable details / sequential execution / cache invalidation / gas-sponsorship fallback; `docs/INVESTOR_FLOW.md` multi-chain deposit bullet rewritten to mention the one-time amount entry and the routing + gas-estimate details panel; the old `MultiChainDepositDrawer` reference is gone.
  - Tests: `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-redeem-panel.test.ts` updated to drop the dead `useApproveUSDC` / `useDeposit` mocks, mock the new `DepositConfirmModal` as a no-op, and pin the new helper copy + the absence of `>Approve USDC<` on first paint; new `apps/web/src/hooks/useParallelChainDeposits.test.ts` covers `computeDepositSplits` (single-chain collapse below the min-split threshold, weight-proportional fan-out above it, empty-weight no-op) and `computeNeedsApprovalPerChain` (insufficient/sufficient/missing-entry cases); new `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-confirm-modal.test.ts` mounts the modal in `open={false}` with the full provider mock surface to lock in that it doesn't throw or render content while closed. E2E `apps/web/e2e/mint-usdc-sepolia.spec.ts::runSplitDeposit` test IDs migrated from `multi-chain-drawer-phase` / `multi-chain-confirm-deposit` / `multi-chain-drawer-content` to the new `deposit-confirm-modal-phase` / `deposit-confirm-submit` / `deposit-confirm-modal-content` so the existing all-chains split-deposit scenario keeps passing.

- [2026-05-23 00:35 UTC+05:30] **Renamed the CI vault-agent commit prefix from `chore(agent)` to `memory(agent)`.** The `commit-results` job in `.github/workflows/vault-agent.yml` and the message builder in `scripts/build-agent-commit-message.mjs` now emit subjects like `memory(agent): mining-manager: wired 2 + opened 1; quality-matrix-manager: created vault + wired 5` (verbose), `memory(agent): refresh memory only`, and the fallback `memory(agent): update agent memory and metadata` — `chore` is no longer used for these auto-pushed deltas because the commits carry only `agents/memory/` + `apps/web/public/agent-metadata/` payloads and "chore" understated the operational signal in `git log`. Updated 4 string literals in `scripts/build-agent-commit-message.mjs` (verbose subject composer, refresh-only fallback, default fallback, and the CLI doc comment), the workflow's static fallback `git commit -m` invocation, and the doc reference in `docs/AGENTS_FRAMEWORK.md`. Updated 3 assertions in `scripts/build-agent-commit-message.test.mjs` (`assert.ok(subject.startsWith("memory(agent): "))` plus the refresh-only and default-fallback `assert.equal` lines) so the existing 8-test suite still pins both the prefix and the per-style subject shapes. No commitlint enforcement exists in this repo, so the non-standard `memory` type is purely a local convention; downstream tooling that filters `git log --grep="^chore(agent)"` (none known) will need updating.

- [2026-05-23 00:27 UTC+05:30] **Tightened the signing-phase copy in the deposit/redeem inline stepper.** `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-redeem-panel.tsx` `InlineTxStepper` now reads `Signing {verb.toLowerCase()}…` / `Authorizing on-chain transaction` instead of `{verb} pending` / `Signed silently by your embedded wallet. No popup needed.` — drops the leaky "embedded wallet" plumbing reference and the defensive "No popup needed" reassurance, and corrects the tense (we are signing in this phase, not pending). No test impact: the existing assertions key off `data-testid="deposit-redeem-stepper"` + `data-phase="signing"`, not the copy.

- [2026-05-23 00:21 UTC+05:30] **Asset Prices mini-charts on the basket detail page now link out to Yahoo Finance**, matching the existing behaviour on the Open Positions table. `apps/web/src/components/baskets/asset-price-panel.tsx` now derives a `yfinanceSymbol` from the on-chain asset name (same predicate as `PositionsTable`: a non-`0x...` decoded symbol), and when present wraps the per-card label in an `<a target="_blank">` anchor pointing at `yahooFinanceQuoteUrl(symbol)` with a `lucide` `ExternalLink` glyph (`h-3 w-3 opacity-60`) and an `aria-label="View {label} on Yahoo Finance"`. Cards for assets without a real ticker (raw `bytes32` ids, etc.) keep the existing plain-text label so the layout doesn't shift. No new lib code — reuses `apps/web/src/lib/yahoo-finance.ts::yahooFinanceQuoteUrl`. No new tests; the symbol-derivation predicate is the same one already validated indirectly via `apps/web/src/components/baskets/positions-table.tsx`.

### Added

- [2026-05-23 00:10 UTC+05:30] **Vault creation now deploys + wires twin baskets on every configured spoke chain in one shot (both the agent MCP and the admin UI), so multi-chain deposit routing works on day one for new baskets without a manual Fuji follow-up.** Previously, the only way to get a name-matched twin on Fuji was the 5-step `cast send` sequence from Phase D of the 2026-05-22 cross-chain fix (`BasketFactory.createBasket` on the spoke, then `setStateRelay`, `setAssets([keccak256("USDC")])`, `MockUSDC.approve` + `topUpReserve`); the `useVaultAddressByName` lookup in the multi-chain deposit drawer would otherwise return `vaultAddress: null` for the new basket on every spoke, and the drawer would correctly skip those chains in `computeDepositSplits` — but that meant no deposits ever reached Fuji for any agent-created vault. Both creation paths now do the spoke fan-out automatically:
  - **MCP `create_vault` (used by `mining-manager` and `quality-matrix-manager` in CI):** `apps/mcps/vault-manager/index.js` `create_vault` now (a) creates the hub vault as before, (b) calls a new helper module `apps/mcps/vault-manager/multichain-create.mjs::discoverSpokeContexts` to enumerate every `config/chains.json` `role: "spoke"` entry whose `apps/web/src/config/<chain>-deployment.json` exists AND whose RPC URL is resolvable from `<RPC_ALIAS_UPPER>_RPC_URL` (matches the keeper's env pattern; `FUJI_RPC_URL` is plumbed through the vault-agent workflow alongside `SEPOLIA_RPC_URL`), then (c) for each discovered spoke runs `createBasket(name, depositFeeBps, redeemFeeBps)` → `setStateRelay(<spoke.stateRelay>)` → `setAssets([keccak256("USDC")])` against the spoke RPC via the same `cast send --private-key ${PRIVATE_KEY} --rpc-url ${spoke.rpcUrl}` plumbing (`castSendOnRpc`). Spokes that are missing either a deployment file or an RPC env var are returned as `{ chain, skipped: true, reason }` records rather than silently dropped, so the agent/operator gets a complete map of where the basket landed and what was deferred. Failures on individual spokes do NOT roll back the hub (the hub vault is already on-chain and agents key off `vaultAddress` = hub for state.json), but every step records the tx hash so retries are surgical: `{ success: false, vaultAddress: "0xPartialTwin", txHashes: { createBasket, setStateRelay }, error: "setAssets reverted on fuji (twin exists at 0xPartialTwin but cannot accept deposits until assets are configured)" }`. The response schema gains a `twins: [{ chain, vaultAddress, success, txHashes, error?, skipped? }]` field; `vaultAddress` (top level) stays the hub address so the existing `scripts/agent-runner.mjs::extractVaultAddressFromCreateVaultResponse` back-compat path (covered by `scripts/agent-runner-create-vault.test.mjs`) is unchanged. New input parameter `deployToSpokes: boolean` (default `true`) lets callers opt out for single-chain creation. Tool description was rewritten to spell out the fan-out behaviour and the stub-asset rationale.
  - **Web admin UI (`/admin/baskets`):** `apps/web/src/app/admin/baskets/page.tsx` `CreateBasketForm` now uses a new hook `apps/web/src/hooks/useCreateMultichainBasket.ts` that orchestrates the same hub → spoke sequence via wagmi + Privy native sponsorship. The hook exposes `state.entries: CreateBasketChainEntry[]` (one per chain) with per-step status (`idle` → `creating` → `wiring_state_relay` → `wiring_assets` → `success` / `error` / `skipped`) plus `vaultAddress`, `createTxHash`, `setStateRelayTxHash`, `setAssetsTxHash`, and `error`. The form gains (a) a `data-testid="admin-create-basket-chains"` checkbox section listing every eligible spoke (hub is fixed-checked; default-selected spokes come from `defaultSpokeTargetsForHub(hubTarget)` which filters `CONFIGURED_DEPLOYMENT_TARGETS` down to chains with both `basketFactory` AND `stateRelay` configured — so chains like `arbitrum-sepolia` that have a factory but no StateRelay wiring won't surface and silently fail mid-flight); (b) a `data-testid="admin-create-basket-progress"` panel that renders a per-chain status row with an icon, label, hub badge, vault address, status copy, and inline error message; (c) a smarter submit button label that reads `Create on N chain(s)` in multi-chain mode and `Create Basket` in the single-chain back-compat path. The progress section makes it obvious which step on which chain failed, so the operator can retry the failed spoke without having to re-create the hub. `useCreateBasket` in `apps/web/src/hooks/useBasketFactory.ts` is left in place for any external callers.
  - **Cross-flow consistency:** both code paths use `keccak256("USDC")` (`0xd6aca1be9729c13d677335161321649cccae6a591554772516700f986f942eaa`) as the spoke stub asset id (`SPOKE_STUB_ASSET_ID` in both `apps/mcps/vault-manager/multichain-create.mjs` and `apps/web/src/hooks/useCreateMultichainBasket.ts`, derived via `viem.keccak256(toBytes("USDC"))` so any drift surfaces immediately at module load) — the same value `script/DeploySpoke.s.sol::_maybeBootstrapSpokeBasket` uses, and the same value Phase D of the 2026-05-22 fix manually pinned on the existing Fuji twins. This means a basket created via the admin form looks identical on-chain to one created via the MCP, and the deposit drawer's `useVaultAddressByName` lookup finds them both.
  - **CI plumbing:** `.github/workflows/vault-agent.yml::Run Agent` env now exports `SEPOLIA_RPC_URL: ${{ secrets.SEPOLIA_RPC_URL }}` and `FUJI_RPC_URL: ${{ secrets.FUJI_RPC_URL }}` (the same secrets used by `keeper.yml` since the 2026-05-22 Envio re-wire). `agents/mcp-servers.json::vault-manager-mcp.envPassthrough` adds `SEPOLIA_RPC_URL`, `FUJI_RPC_URL`, and `ARBITRUM_SEPOLIA_RPC_URL` for symmetry (currently the agent runner's `spawnMcpClient` already spreads `process.env` into the MCP subprocess, so `envPassthrough` is documentation-only — but the list is the source of truth for which env vars the MCP cares about).
  - **Tests:** new `apps/mcps/vault-manager/multichain-create.test.mjs` (10 node-test cases) covers `SPOKE_STUB_ASSET_ID` pinning, the `<alias>_RPC_URL` env-key derivation, three `discoverSpokeContexts` branches (configured spokes returned, missing RPC env reported, incomplete deployment config reported), and five `deploySpokeTwin` paths (happy path with exact arg shapes for all three `cast send` calls; createBasket revert short-circuits before setStateRelay; setAssets revert surfaces the partial twin address so it's recoverable; missing `BasketCreated` log returns a descriptive error; cast errors are run through the injected redactor so the private key never leaks). New `apps/web/src/hooks/useCreateMultichainBasket.test.ts` (3 vitest cases) covers the `defaultSpokeTargetsForHub` filter (excludes the hub itself, excludes spokes without a StateRelay) and pins `keccak256("USDC") === SPOKE_STUB_ASSET_ID` so any viem/keccak drift fails at CI time. All existing `scripts/agent-runner-create-vault.test.mjs` (11 tests) and `apps/web/src/hooks/useVaultAddressByName.test.ts` (4 tests) still pass.
  - **Docs:** `docs/CROSS_CHAIN_COORDINATION.md` "Per-chain twin basket requirement" section will be updated in a follow-up edit to note that the manual `cast send` recipe is now only needed for back-filling existing single-chain baskets; net-new baskets fan out automatically. `AGENTS.md` / per-agent skills unchanged — the MCP tool signature is back-compat (extra optional `deployToSpokes` field) and the agent prompts already say "create a vault if none exists".

### Fixed

- [2026-05-23 00:55 UTC+05:30] **Basket trade-panel primary button now correctly reverts to the "needs approval" state after a deposit consumes the allowance.** `apps/web/src/components/baskets/deposit-redeem-panel.tsx` previously only called `refetchAllowance()` + `refetchSimDeposit()` on `approveReceipt.isSuccess`, never on `depositReceipt.isSuccess` — so when the user typed a new amount immediately after a deposit, the cached allowance was stale and the button could mis-render as "Deposit" instead of "Approve USDC" even though the on-chain allowance had been spent. The deposit-success effect now also resets `approvalInFlightAmount` to `0n` and re-runs both refetches. (Largely a safety net now that the unified `DepositConfirmModal` owns the approve+deposit pipeline and invalidates the allowance cache per chain after each deposit via `invalidateAllowanceCache` in `useParallelChainDeposits`, but kept because the panel still drives a `useUSDCAllowance` read for the redeem path's balance display.)

- [2026-05-22 23:55 UTC+05:30] **Cross-chain deposit routing actually splits across Sepolia + Fuji now, instead of silently sending 100% to Sepolia.** Five compounding issues stacked on top of each other meant the multi-chain deposit drawer never produced a real Fuji deposit since the 2026-05-21 redeploy: (1) both agent vaults on Sepolia (`Minestarters ML Picks 0x4dcd…a0ba`, `Minestarters Quality Matrix 0xbd7e…41f3`) had `stateRelay() == 0x0` — the deposit weight gate in `BasketVault.deposit` L198–L201 was silently bypassed because `address(stateRelay) != address(0)` was false; (2) the keeper had only ever posted single-chain weights to Sepolia's StateRelay (`getRoutingWeights()` returned just `[Sepolia → 10000bps]`) and had never written to Fuji's StateRelay at all (`localWeight() == 0`, table empty) — the post-redeploy `npm run keeper:once` ran successfully against both chains but the cron-tick-pusher hadn't fired in ~36h, so no further epochs landed; (3) the multi-chain deposit drawer in `apps/web/src/components/baskets/multi-chain-deposit-drawer.tsx` L127–L137 passed the SAME `vaultAddress` prop to every chain in `vaultMappings` (Sepolia address forwarded to Fuji), which would revert on Fuji because that address doesn't exist there — `useParallelChainDeposits.execute` would log the failure and move on; (4) the agent vaults (`Minestarters ML Picks`, `Minestarters Quality Matrix`) only existed on Sepolia, so even if the drawer used per-chain addresses there was nothing on Fuji to target; (5) freshly created spoke baskets need `setAssets(bytes32[])` with at least one entry to pass `BasketVault.deposit`'s `require(assets.length > 0, "No assets configured")` check (the bootstrap basket does this via `keccak256("USDC")` in `script/DeploySpoke.s.sol` L196–L198, but `BasketFactory.createBasket` alone does not set assets). Fixes:
  - **On-chain (Sepolia, owner = deployer wallet `0x36716C…9713`):** `setStateRelay(0xA2DcF0…cc7d)` on `0x4dcd…a0ba` (tx `0x5b89de33…b849c58`) and `0xbd7e…41f3` (tx `0x084092a1…fbdc6f5`) — wires both Sepolia agent vaults to the deployed `StateRelay`. Both reads now return the live StateRelay address and the deposit weight gate is active.
  - **On-chain (Fuji, owner = deployer wallet):** deployed twin baskets `Minestarters ML Picks → 0x6180f8d6cdd3f5d42d00130b52ab635a2d618f53` (createBasket tx `0xde70614e…a90aad12`) and `Minestarters Quality Matrix → 0x435847fa2f0c91c6d39067ab85c0cd9234d2b979` (tx `0x2939c8f4…c9051471`), both via `BasketFactory(0xb79721…3c29).createBasket(name, 50, 50)` matching the Sepolia originals' 50bps deposit + 50bps redeem fees. For each twin: `setStateRelay(0xF6fa7d…a515)`, `setAssets([keccak256("USDC")])` (stub asset id, same pattern as `script/DeploySpoke.s.sol::_maybeBootstrapSpokeBasket` since Fuji has no oracle adapter deployed), and `MockUSDC.approve` + `topUpReserve(1_000_000_000)` to pre-seed each with 1,000 mUSDC of idle reserve. Final Fuji state: bootstrap basket `0x40a97c…eFd1` at $100k (unchanged), ML Picks twin at $1k, Quality Matrix twin at $1k, total Fuji idle = $102k vs Sepolia idle $4k → keeper assigns Sepolia `9622bps` / Fuji `378bps` (inverse-idle formula via `services/keeper/src/computeRoutingWeights.ts`).
  - **Keeper:** ran `npm run keeper:once` twice — once after Phase A to refresh weights with the new Sepolia StateRelay wiring, once after Phase D to pick up the new Fuji twin baskets (vault count went from 3 to 5 across chains). Both `StateRelay.getRoutingWeights()` calls now return the same two-chain table on Sepolia and Fuji. The recurring cron-tick-pusher gap is documented for operator follow-up (gh CLI auth not available in this session — verify via `gh run list --workflow=cron-tick-pusher.yml --limit 10` and re-arm with `gh workflow run cron-tick-pusher.yml` if the self-dispatch loop died).
  - **Web app:** new hook `apps/web/src/hooks/useVaultAddressByName.ts` resolves per-chain twin vault addresses by basket name. For each entry in `CONFIGURED_DEPLOYMENT_TARGETS`, it calls `BasketFactory.getAllBaskets()` then `multicall`s `BasketVault.name()` on every result, matching case-insensitively after `trim().toLowerCase()`. The current chain's address is pinned to the `referenceVaultAddress` prop (avoids the race where a fresh `createBasket` hasn't been indexed yet). Chains without a twin return `vaultAddress: null` so the drawer can surface "this basket is not deployed on Fuji" instead of forwarding a Sepolia address to Fuji and silently failing.
